CanvasChain Symphony – Minting Service\n//! --------------------------------------\n//! Factory & Strategy pattern–powered NFT minting logic.\n//! Creates multilayer NFTs, signs mint payloads with a pluggable crypto\n//! backend and broadcasts events onto the internal event-bus.\n//!\n//! This file purposefully hides transport concerns (gRPC, HTTP, etc.); the\n//! factory is a pure domain component that can be wired into whatever\n//! outer-layer the micro-service exposes.\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::sync::Arc;\nuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse tokio::sync::RwLock;\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Domain Types\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Human-readable, multi-layer NFT metadata sent by the client.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct MintRequest {\n    /// Wallet address of the creator in bech32 / hex format (depends on chain).\n    pub creator_wallet: String,\n    /// An ordered list of media layers that compose the artwork.\n    pub layers: Vec<NftLayer>,\n    /// Royalty basis-points (1/100 of a percent).\n    pub royalty_bps: u16,\n    /// Arbitrary on-chain attributes (e.g. color_palette → “pastel”).\n    pub attributes: HashMap<String, String>,\n    /// Number used once to avoid replay attacks.\n    pub nonce: u64,\n}\n\n/// Individual layer of the final NFT.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct NftLayer {\n    pub id: u32,\n    /// IPFS / Arweave / S3 URI\n    pub uri: String,\n}\n\n/// Normalised payload that the chain client can send to the runtime.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct MintPayload {\n    pub idempotency_key: Uuid,\n    pub creator_wallet: String,\n    pub metadata_cid: String,\n    pub royalty_bps: u16,\n    pub signature: Vec<u8>,\n}\n\n/// Successful on-chain minting result.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct NftReceipt {\n    pub token_id: u64,\n    pub tx_hash: String,\n    pub signature: Vec<u8>,\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Error Handling\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum FactoryError {\n    #[error(\"invalid royalty basis points: {0}\")]\n    InvalidRoyalty(u16),\n    #[error(\"mint payload validation error: {0}\")]\n    Validation(String),\n    #[error(\"crypto error: {0}\")]\n    Crypto(#[from] CryptoError),\n    #[error(\"blockchain client error: {0}\")]\n    Blockchain(#[from] BlockchainError),\n    #[error(\"event bus error: {0}\")]\n    EventBus(#[from] EventBusError),\n}\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum CryptoError {\n    #[error(\"unsupported curve\")]\n    UnsupportedCurve,\n    #[error(\"signature generation failed\")]\n    SignFailure,\n}\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum BlockchainError {\n    #[error(\"node unreachable\")]\n    NodeUnreachable,\n    #[error(\"transaction rejected: {0}\")]\n    TransactionRejected(String),\n}\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum EventBusError {\n    #[error(\"publish failed\")]\n    PublishFailed,\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Traits (Strategy / Port abstractions)\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Unified crypto-signer strategy so we can swap Ed25519, BLS, PQ, etc.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait CryptoSigner: Send + Sync {\n    /// ‘Human’ name of the algorithm (ed25519, bls12381, falcon, …).\n    fn algorithm(&self) -> &'static str;\n\n    /// Public identifier derived from the secret key (hex / bech32).\n    fn public_key(&self) -> String;\n\n    /// Sign arbitrary message bytes.\n    async fn sign(&self, message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CryptoError>;\n}\n\n/// Port for whatever chain client we’re using (substrate, cosmos, homework-chain).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait BlockchainClient: Send + Sync {\n    async fn submit_mint(&self, payload: MintPayload) -> Result<String, BlockchainError>;\n    async fn latest_token_id(&self) -> Result<u64, BlockchainError>;\n}\n\n/// Simplistic event bus publisher (fan-out / kafka / nats / anything).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher: Send + Sync {\n    async fn publish<M: Serialize + Send + Sync>(&self, topic: &str, msg: &M) -> Result<(), EventBusError>;\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// NFT Factory (Concrete implementation of the Factory pattern)\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Shared reference-counted pointer to immutable factory configuration.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FactoryConfig {\n    /// Maximum allowed royalty (bps). Prevents creator mistakes.\n    pub max_royalty_bps: u16,\n    /// Event bus topic for successful mints.\n    pub event_topic_success: String,\n    /// Event bus topic for failed mints.\n    pub event_topic_failure: String,\n}\n\npub struct NftFactory<S, C, E>\nwhere\n    S: CryptoSigner,\n    C: BlockchainClient,\n    E: EventPublisher,\n{\n    signer: Arc<S>,\n    chain: Arc<C>,\n    bus: Arc<E>,\n    config: Arc<FactoryConfig>,\n    // optionally keep some internal state (metrics, counters, etc.)\n    minted_counter: RwLock<u64>,\n}\n\nimpl<S, C, E> NftFactory<S, C, E>\nwhere\n    S: CryptoSigner + 'static,\n    C: BlockchainClient + 'static,\n    E: EventPublisher + 'static,\n{\n    pub fn new(signer: Arc<S>, chain: Arc<C>, bus: Arc<E>, config: FactoryConfig) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            signer,\n            chain,\n            bus,\n            config: Arc::new(config),\n            minted_counter: RwLock::new(0),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Validate, sign, submit and broadcast the mint request.\n    #[instrument(skip_all, level = \"info\", fields(creator = %req.creator_wallet))]\n    pub async fn mint_nft(&self, req: MintRequest) -> Result<NftReceipt, FactoryError> {\n        self.validate_request(&req)?;\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 1. Serialize metadata to JSON; in reality we would push to IPFS/Arweave\n        // and obtain a CID, we’ll fake it by hashing the JSON.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let metadata_json = serde_json::to_vec(&req)\n            .map_err(|e| FactoryError::Validation(format!(\"metadata serialization: {e}\")))?;\n\n        let metadata_cid = hex::encode(blake3::hash(&metadata_json).as_bytes());\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 2. Build chain payload and sign it with the selected algorithm.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let idempotency_key = Uuid::new_v4();\n        let mut payload_bytes = Vec::new();\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(idempotency_key.as_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(metadata_cid.as_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(&req.royalty_bps.to_le_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(req.creator_wallet.as_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(&req.nonce.to_le_bytes());\n\n        let signature = self.signer.sign(&payload_bytes).await?;\n\n        let payload = MintPayload {\n            idempotency_key,\n            creator_wallet: req.creator_wallet.clone(),\n            metadata_cid,\n            royalty_bps: req.royalty_bps,\n            signature: signature.clone(),\n        };\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 3. Submit mint extrinsic / transaction to the chain.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let tx_hash = match self.chain.submit_mint(payload.clone()).await {\n            Ok(h) => h,\n            Err(e) => {\n                // fire & forget failure event\n                let _ = self\n                    .bus\n                    .publish(\n                        &self.config.event_topic_failure,\n                        &MintFailureEvent::from_request(&req, &format!(\"{e}\")),\n                    )\n                    .await;\n                return Err(e.into());\n            }\n        };\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 4. Retrieve token ID (optimistic assumption we mint sequentially).\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let token_id = self.chain.latest_token_id().await?;\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 5. Bump metrics & broadcast success event.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        {\n            let mut counter = self.minted_counter.write().await;\n            *counter += 1;\n        }\n\n        let receipt = NftReceipt {\n            token_id,\n            tx_hash: tx_hash.clone(),\n            signature,\n        };\n\n        self.bus\n            .publish(&self.config.event_topic_success, &receipt)\n            .await?;\n\n        info!(token_id, tx_hash, \"NFT minted successfully\");\n        Ok(receipt)\n    }\n\n    // -------------------\n    // internal helpers\n    // -------------------\n    fn validate_request(&self, req: &MintRequest) -> Result<(), FactoryError> {\n        if req.royalty_bps > self.config.max_royalty_bps {\n            return Err(FactoryError::InvalidRoyalty(req.royalty_bps));\n        }\n        if req.layers.is_empty() {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"at least one layer required\".into()));\n        }\n        if req.creator_wallet.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"creator_wallet is empty\".into()));\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Event Definitions\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\nstruct MintFailureEvent {\n    pub creator_wallet: String,\n    pub timestamp_ms: u128,\n    pub reason: String,\n}\n\nimpl MintFailureEvent {\n    fn from_request(req: &MintRequest, reason: &str) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            creator_wallet: req.creator_wallet.clone(),\n            timestamp_ms: SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .expect(\"system time ok\")\n                .as_millis(),\n            reason: reason.to_string(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Dummy/Mock implementations behind ‘test’ feature so we can run unit tests.\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::sync::Mutex;\n\n    // --------------------\n    // Mock crypto signer\n    // --------------------\n    struct MockSigner;\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl CryptoSigner for MockSigner {\n        fn algorithm(&self) -> &'static str {\n            \"mocked-ed25519\"\n        }\n\n        fn public_key(&self) -> String {\n            \"deadbeef\".to_string()\n        }\n\n        async fn sign(&self, message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CryptoError> {\n            Ok(blake3::hash(message).as_bytes().to_vec())\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------\n    // Mock chain client\n    // --------------------\n    struct MockChain {\n        counter: Mutex<u64>,\n    }\n\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl BlockchainClient for MockChain {\n        async fn submit_mint(&self, _payload: MintPayload) -> Result<String, BlockchainError> {\n            let mut c = self.counter.lock().unwrap();\n            *c += 1;\n            Ok(format!(\"0x{:x}\", *c))\n        }\n\n        async fn latest_token_id(&self) -> Result<u64, BlockchainError> {\n            let c = self.counter.lock().unwrap();\n            Ok(*c)\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------\n    // Mock event bus\n    // --------------------\n    struct MockBus;\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl EventPublisher for MockBus {\n        async fn publish<M: Serialize + Send + Sync>(\n            &self,\n            topic: &str,\n            msg: &M,\n        ) -> Result<(), EventBusError> {\n            let payload = serde_json::to_string(msg).unwrap();\n            debug!(topic, payload, \"mock bus publish\");\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_mint_success() {\n        let signer = Arc::new(MockSigner);\n        let chain = Arc::new(MockChain {\n Marketplace ‑ Listing Manager\n//!\n//! This module is responsible for orchestrating the full life-cycle of NFT\n//! listings in the CanvasChain Symphony marketplace micro-service.  \n//! It follows Repository + Event Driven patterns to keep storage concerns and\n//! side-effects clearly separated from business logic.\n//!\n//! Responsibilities\n//! * Create / update / cancel fixed-price listings\n//! * Atomically settle purchases (reserve → transfer → close)\n//! * Publish `MarketplaceEvent`s on the internal event bus\n//! * Provide a type-safe façade for gRPC handlers & GraphQL resolvers\n//!\n//! Note: In production, `ListingRepository` would be backed by Postgres or\n//! RocksDB; here we ship an in-memory implementation for completeness.\n\n#![allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::Duration,\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse rust_decimal::Decimal;\nuse tokio::{\n    sync::{broadcast, RwLock},\n    time::timeout,\n};\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\n// region: ───── Domain Types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct ListingId(Uuid);\n\nimpl ListingId {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self(Uuid::new_v4())\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct AccountId(Uuid);\n\nimpl AccountId {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self(Uuid::new_v4())\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]\npub enum ListingStatus {\n    Active,\n    Reserved { buyer: AccountId, reserved_at: DateTime<Utc> },\n    Sold,\n    Cancelled,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Listing {\n    pub id: ListingId,\n    pub owner: AccountId,\n    pub nft_contract: String,\n    pub token_id: String,\n    pub price: Decimal,\n    pub status: ListingStatus,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n// endregion: ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// region: ───── Errors ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ListingError {\n    #[error(\"Listing not found\")]\n    NotFound,\n    #[error(\"Listing is not active\")]\n    NotActive,\n    #[error(\"Reserved listing. Only buyer {0:?} can proceed\")]\n    Reserved(AccountId),\n    #[error(\"Listing already sold\")]\n    AlreadySold,\n    #[error(\"Listing already cancelled\")]\n    AlreadyCancelled,\n    #[error(\"Repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repository(String),\n    #[error(\"Event dispatch failed: {0}\")]\n    EventDispatch(String),\n    #[error(\"Operation timed-out\")]\n    Timeout,\n}\n\n// ListingRepository: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn insert(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError>;\n    async fn get(&self, id: ListingId) -> Result<Listing, ListingError>;\n    async fn update(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError>;\n    async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Listing>, ListingError>;\n}\n\n// endregion: ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// region: ───── In-Memory Repository (example impl) ───────────────────────────\n\n/// Simple, thread-safe in-memory repository.  \n/// In production this would be swapped for a SQL or KV store implementation.\npub struct InMemoryListingRepository {\n    /// Internal map guarded by a read-write lock\n    listings: RwLock<HashMap<ListingId, Listing>>,\n}\n\nimpl InMemoryListingRepository {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            listings: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl ListingRepository for InMemoryListingRepository {\n    #[instrument(skip(self, listing))]\n    async fn insert(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        let mut map = self.listings.write().await;\n        map.insert(listing.id, listing);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    async fn get(&self, id: ListingId) -> Result<Listing, ListingError> {\n        let map = self.listings.read().await;\n        map.get(&id).cloned().ok_or(ListingError::NotFound)\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self, listing))]\n    async fn update(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        let mut map = self.listings.write().await;\n        map.insert(listing.id, listing);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Listing>, ListingError> {\n        let map = self.listings.read().await;\n        Ok(map\n            .values()\n            .filter(|l| matches!(l.status, ListingStatus::Active))\n            .cloned()\n            .collect())\n    }\n}\n\n// endregion: ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// region: ───── Event Bus Types ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum ────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct ListingManager<R: ListingRepository> {\n    repo: Arc<R>,\n    events: broadcast::Sender<MarketplaceEvent>,\n}\n\n/// Upper bound for potentially blocking database operations.\nconst DB_OP_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3);\n\nimpl<R: ListingRepository> ListingManager<R> {\n    pub fn new(repo: Arc<R>) -> Self {\n        let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(256);\n        Self { repo, events: tx }\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to marketplace events.\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<MarketplaceEvent> {\n        self.events.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn create_listing(\n        &self,\n        owner: AccountId,\n owner,\n            nft_contract,\n            token_id,\n            price,\n            status: ListingStatus::Active,\n            created_at: Utc::now(),\n            updated_at: Utc::now(),\n        };\n\n        timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.insert(listing.clone()))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)?\n            ?;\n\n        self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::ListingCreated(listing))?;\n\n        Ok(listing.id)\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn reserve_listing(\n        &self,\n .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??\n            ;\n\n        match listing.status {\n            ListingStatus::Active => {\n                listing.status = ListingStatus::Reserved {\n                    buyer,\n                    reserved_at: Utc::now(),\n                };\n                listing.updated_at = Utc::now();\n            }\n            ListingStatus::Reserved { buyer: original, .. } if original == buyer => {\n                // Idempotent\n                return Ok(());\n            }\n            ListingStatus::Reserved { buyer: other, .. } => {\n                return Err(ListingError::Reserved(other));\n            }\n            ListingStatus::Sold => return Err(ListingError::AlreadySold),\n            ListingStatus::Cancelled => return Err(ListingError::AlreadyCancelled),\n        }\n\n        timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.update(listing.clone()))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??\n            ;\n\n        self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::ListingReserved { listing_id, buyer })?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn cancel_listing(&self, listing_id: ListingId, caller: AccountId) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        let mut listing = timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.get(listing_id))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??\n            ;\n\n        if listing.owner != caller {\n            return Err(ListingError::Repository(\"Only owner can cancel\".into()));\n        }\n\n        match listing.status {\n            ListingStatus::Sold => return Err(ListingError::AlreadySold),\n            ListingStatus::Cancelled => return Err(ListingError::AlreadyCancelled),\n            _ => {}\n        }\n\n        listing.status = ListingStatus::Cancelled;\n        listing.updated_at = Utc::now();\n\n        timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.update(listing.clone()))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??\n            ;\n\n        self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::ListingCancelled(listing_id))?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn purchase_listing(\n        &self,\n        listing_id: ListingId,\n        buyer: AccountId,\n    ) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        let mut listing = timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.get(listing_id))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??\n            ;\n\n        // Pre-flight state validation\n        match &listing.status {\n            ListingStatus::Active => {\n                // We allow direct purchase without reservation\n            }\n            ListingStatus::Reserved { buyer: reserved_buyer, .. } if *reserved_buyer == buyer => {}\n            ListingStatus::Reserved { buyer: reserved_buyer, .. } => {\n                return Err(ListingError::Reserved(*reserved_buyer));\n            }\n            ListingStatus::Sold => return Err(ListingError::AlreadySold),\n            ListingStatus::Cancelled => return Err(ListingError::AlreadyCancelled),\n        }\n\n        // TODO: integrate with payment processor / smart contract to transfer funds & NFT\n\n        // When settlement succeeds:\n        listing.status = ListingStatus::Sold;\n        listing.updated_at = Utc::now();\n\n        timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.update(listing.clone()))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??\n            ;\n\n        self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::ListingPurchased { listing_id, buyer })?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn get_listing(&self, listing_id: ListingId) -> Result<Listing, ListingError> {\n        timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.get(listing_id))\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)?\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Listing>, ListingError> {\n        timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.list_active())\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)?\n    }\n\n    fn dispatch_event(&self, evt: MarketplaceEvent) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        self.events\n            .send(evt)\n            .map(|_| ())\n            .map_err(|e| ListingError::EventDispatch(e.to_string()))\n    }\n}\n\n// endregion: ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// region: ───── Tests Finalized,\n    Cancelled,\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Auction {\n    pub id: AuctionId,\n    pub nft_id: NftId,\n    pub seller_id: UserId,\n    pub reserve_price: u128,\n    pub start_time: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub end_time: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub status: AuctionStatus,\n    pub highest_bid: Option<Bid>,\n}\n\nimpl Auction {\n    /// Returns whether the auction is open for bids.\n    pub fn is_active(&self, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.status, AuctionStatus::Active)\n            && now >= self.start_time\n            && now < self.end_time\n    }\n\n    /// Returns whether auction should be finalized (elapsed or cancelled).\n    pub fn should_finalize(&self, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.status, AuctionStatus::Active) && now >= self.end_time\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                    Store                                   */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/// Storage abstraction. Can be backed by Postgres, Redis, RocksDB, etc.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait AuctionStore: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn insert(&self, auction: Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError>;\n    async fn update(&self, auction: &Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError>;\n    async fn get(&self, id: AuctionId) -> Result<Option<Auction>, EngineError>;\n    async fn all_due_for_finalize(\n        &self,\n        now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<AuctionId>, EngineError>;\n}\n\n/* --------------------------- In-memory implementation --------------------------- */\n\ntype AuctionMap = Arc<RwLock<HashMap<AuctionId, Auction>>>;\n\n/// An in-memory, thread-safe store backed by a `HashMap`.\n/// Meant for tests / local development, *not* production.\n#[derive(Clone, Default)]\npub struct MemoryAuctionStore {\n    map: AuctionMap,\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl AuctionStore for MemoryAuctionStore {\n    async fn insert(&self, auction: Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let mut map = self.map.write().await;\n        map.insert(auction.id, auction);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn update(&self, auction: &Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let mut map = self.map.write().await;\n        if let Some(a) = map.get_mut(&auction.id) {\n            *a = auction.clone();\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound)\n        }\n    }\n\n    async fn get(&self, id: AuctionId) -> Result<Option<Auction>, EngineError> {\n        let map = self.map.read().await;\n        Ok(map.get(&id).cloned())\n    }\n\n    async fn all_due_for_finalize(\n        &self,\n        now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<AuctionId>, EngineError> {\n        let map = self.map.read().await;\n        Ok(map\n            .values()\n            .filter(|a| a.should_finalize(now))\n            .map(|a| a.id)\n            .collect())\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Event definitions                            */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum AuctionEvent {\n    AuctionCreated(AuctionId),\n    BidPlaced {\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        bidder_id: UserId,\n        amount: u128,\n    },\n    AuctionFinalized {\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        winner: Option<UserId>,\n        amount: Option<u128>,\n    },\n    AuctionCancelled(AuctionId),\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Error handling                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum EngineError {\n    #[error(\"auction not found\")]\n    AuctionNotFound,\n    #[error(\"auction is not active\")]\n    AuctionNotActive,\n    #[error(\"bid below reserve price\")]\n    ReserveNotMet,\n    #[error(\"bid increment too low\")]\n    IncrementTooLow,\n    #[error(\"seller cannot bid on own auction\")]\n    SellerCannotBid,\n    #[error(\"auction already finalized or cancelled\")]\n    InvalidAuctionState,\n    #[error(\"storage error: {0}\")]\n    Storage(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n    #[error(\"broadcast error\")]\n    Broadcast,\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                AuctionEngine                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct AuctionEngine<S: AuctionStore> {\n    store: Arc<S>,\n    /// Minimum bid increment (e.g. 1% of current highest) expressed as basis points.\n    min_increment_bps: u16,\n    /// Tokio broadcast channel for events.\n    event_tx: broadcast::Sender<AuctionEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: AuctionStore> AuctionEngine<S> {\n    /// Create an engine using the provided store.\n    pub fn new(store: S, min_increment_bps: u16) -> Self {\n        let (event_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(1024);\n        Self {\n            store: Arc::new(store),\n            min_increment_bps,\n            event_tx,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Convenience helper for tests/local dev.\n    pub fn with_memory_store(config: EngineConfig) -> Self {\n        Self::new(MemoryAuctionStore::default(), config.min_increment_bps)\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to auction events (fire-and-forget).\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<AuctionEvent> {\n        self.event_tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- API methods ----------------------------- */\n\n    /// Creates an auction and stores it. Emits `AuctionCreated`.\n    pub async fn create_auction(\n        &self,\n        params: CreateAuction,\n    ) -> Result<AuctionId, EngineError> {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let auction = Auction {\n            id: AuctionId::new(),\n            nft_id: params.nft_id,\n            seller_id: params.seller_id,\n            reserve_price: params.reserve_price,\n            start_time: now,\n            end_time: now + params.duration,\n            status: AuctionStatus::Active,\n            highest_bid: None,\n        };\n        self.store.insert(auction.clone()).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::AuctionCreated(auction.id))?;\n        Ok(auction.id)\n    }\n\n    /// Places a new bid on an active auction.\n    pub async fn place_bid(\n        &self,\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        bidder_id: UserId,\n        amount: u128,\n    ) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let mut auction = match self.store.get(auction_id).await? {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound),\n        };\n\n        if !auction.is_active(now) {\n            return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotActive);\n        }\n\n        if bidder_id == auction.seller_id {\n            return Err(EngineError::SellerCannotBid);\n        }\n\n        // First bid must be >= reserve.\n        if auction.highest_bid.is_none() && amount < auction.reserve_price {\n            return Err(EngineError::ReserveNotMet);\n        }\n\n        // Ensure minimum increment.\n        if let Some(ref high) = auction.highest_bid {\n            let min_required =\n                high.amount + Self::calc_increment(high.amount, self.min_increment_bps);\n            if amount < min_required {\n                return Err(EngineError::IncrementTooLow);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // TODO: anti-sniping extension window can be applied here.\n\n        auction.highest_bid = Some(Bid {\n            bidder_id,\n            amount,\n            timestamp: now,\n        });\n\n        self.store.update(&auction).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::BidPlaced {\n            auction_id,\n            bidder_id,\n            amount,\n        })?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Cancels an auction by its seller before any bids are placed.\n    pub async fn cancel_auction(\n        &self,\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        requester: UserId,\n    ) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let mut auction = match self.store.get(auction_id).await? {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound),\n        };\n\n        if auction.seller_id != requester {\n            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAuctionState);\n        }\n\n        if auction.highest_bid.is_some() || !matches!(auction.status, AuctionStatus::Active) {\n            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAuctionState);\n        }\n\n        auction.status = AuctionStatus::Cancelled;\n        self.store.update(&auction).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::AuctionCancelled(auction_id))?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Finalize an auction that reached its end time. Returns the winner if any.\n    pub async fn finalize_auction(\n        &self,\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n    ) -> Result<Option<(UserId, u128)>, EngineError> {\n        let mut auction = match self.store.get(auction_id).await? {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound),\n        };\n\n        if !auction.should_finalize(Utc::now()) {\n            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAuctionState);\n        }\n\n        auction.status = AuctionStatus::Finalized;\n        let winner_info = auction\n            .highest_bid\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|b| (b.bidder_id, b.amount));\n\n        self.store.update(&auction).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::AuctionFinalized {\n            auction_id,\n            winner: winner_info.map(|t| t.0),\n            amount: winner_info.map(|t| t.1),\n        })?;\n\n        // TODO: trigger settlement workflow (escrow smart contract, royalties, etc.)\n        Ok(winner_info)\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- Internals ------------------------------ */\n\n    fn emit(&self, evt: AuctionEvent) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        self.event_tx\n            .send(evt)\n            .map_err(|_| EngineError::Broadcast)\n            .map(|_| ())\n    }\n\n    fn calc_increment(current: u128, bps: u16) -> u128 {\n        // basis points: 1 bps = 0.01 %\n        (current * bps as u128) / 10_000u128\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- Finalize loop ---------------------------- */\n\n    /// Spawns a Tokio task that periodically finalizes elapsed auctions.\n    ///\n    /// NOTE: In a clustered deployment only the leader instance should run the\n    /// loop to avoid double-finalization.  This is achieved via distributed\n    /// locks (e.g. etcd/consul) at the service orchestrator layer.\n    pub fn spawn_finalize_loop(self, interval: StdDuration) -> JoinHandle<()> {\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let mut ticker = tokio::time::interval(interval);\n            loop {\n                ticker.tick().await;\n                let now = Utc::now();\n                match self.store.all_due_for_finalize(now).await {\n                    Ok(ids) => {\n                        for id in ids {\n                            // best effort, log errors\n                            if let Err(e) = self.finalize_auction(id).await {\n                                tracing::warn!(\n                                    auction_id = ?id,\n                                    error = ?e,\n                                    \"failed to finalize auction\"\n                                );\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                    Err(e) => tracing::error!(error = ?e, \"finalize loop failed to fetch auctions\"),\n                }\n            }\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                              Engine configuration                          */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EngineConfig {\n    pub min_increment_bps: u16,\n}\n\nimpl Default for EngineConfig {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self { min_increment_bps: 50 } // 0.5%\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                   Display                                  */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nimpl fmt::Display for AuctionId {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        self.0.fmt(f)\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":494,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

Key Manager\n//!\n//! The Key Manager is responsible for generating, encrypting, storing and\n//! retrieving private keys for the wallet–service.  It supports multiple\n//! signing curves (Ed25519 and Secp256k1 today; BLS can be added behind a\n//! feature-flag) and uses Argon2 + ChaCha20-Poly1305 to protect keys at\n//! rest.\n//!\n//! The module follows a Strategy pattern where each curve implements the\n//! `SigningCurve` trait.  A very small, file–based keystore is provided for\n//! demo/PoC purposes; in production an HSM or KMS implementation should\n//! instead be plugged in via the same trait.\n//!\n//! NOTE: All public functions are `async` to keep the API uniform even\n//! though the current implementation is I/O–bound only.\n\nuse std::{\n    fs::{self, File},\n    io::Write,\n    path::{Path, signature algorithms.\n///\n/// New curves (BLS, PQ, …) can be enabled behind a Cargo feature flag and\n/// added here without touching the rest of the service.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum CryptoAlgorithm {\n    Ed25519,\n    Secp256k1,\n}\n\n/// A minimal, curve-agnostic keypair.\n///\n/// The concrete key material is stored encrypted on disk; we only keep the\n/// public key and metadata in memory.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct WalletMeta {\n    pub id:              Uuid,\n    pub algo:            CryptoAlgorithm,\n    pub public_key:      Vec<u8>,\n    pub encrypted_sk:    Vec<u8>,\n    pub enc_salt:        Vec<u8>,\n    pub enc_nonce:       Vec<u8>,\n    pub created_at:      DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n/// Top-level manager.\n///\n/// Internally uses a `KeyStore` implementation and a registry of available\n/// signing curves.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct KeyManager {\n    keystore: Arc<dyn KeyStore>,\n    curves:   CurveRegistry,\n}\n\nimpl KeyManager {\n    /// Create a new manager with the default [`FileKeyStore`].\n    pub async fn new<P: AsRef<Path>>(file: P) -> Result<Self, KeyManagerError> {\n        let ks = FileKeyStore::open(file).await?;\n        let mut registry = CurveRegistry::default();\n        registry.register(Box::<Ed25519Curve>::default());\n        registry.register(Box::<Secp256k1Curve>::default());\n\n        Ok(Self {\n            keystore: Arc::new(ks),\n            curves:   registry,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Creates and persists a            .curves\n            .get(algo)\n            .ok_or(KeyManagerError::UnsupportedAlgorithm)?;\n\n        // 1) Generate a fresh keypair\n        let (sk, pk) = curve.generate()?;\n\n        // 2) Encrypt the secret key with Argon2 + ChaCha20-Poly1305\n        let (ciphertext, salt, nonce) = encrypt_secret(password, &sk)?;\n\n        // 3) Store on disk\n        let wallet = WalletMeta {\n            id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n            algo,\n            public_key: pk,\n            encrypted_sk: ciphertext,\n            enc_salt: salt.to_vec(),\n            enc_nonce: nonce.to_vec(),\n            created_at: Utc::now(),\n        };\n\n        self.keystore.put(&wallet).await?;\n        Ok(wallet)\n    }\n\n    /// Unlock a wallet, returning the raw secret key in The caller is responsible for zeroizing the\n    /// buffer after use.\n    pub async fn unlock_wallet(\n        &self,\n        id: Uuid,\n        password: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<UnlockedWallet, KeyManagerError> {\n        let meta = self\n            .keystore\n            .get(id)\n            .await?\n            .ok_or(KeyManagerError::WalletNotFound)?;\n\n        let curve = self\n            .curves\n            .get(meta.algo)\n            .ok_or(KeyManagerError::UnsupportedAlgorithm)?;\n\n        let sk = decrypt_secret(\n            password,\n            &meta.encrypted_sk,\n            &meta.enc_salt,\n            &meta.enc_nonce,\n        )?;\n\n        Ok(UnlockedWallet {\n            meta,\n            secret_key: sk,\n            curve,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Sign an arbitrary message with the given wallet.\n    pub async fn sign(\n        &self,\n        wallet: &UnlockedWallet,\n        message: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError> {\n        wallet.curve.sign(&wallet.secret_key, message)\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Unlocked wallet (ephemeral)\n// secret: &[u8], message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError>;\n\n    /// Verify a signature – primarily for unit tests.\n    #[allow(dead_code)]\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError>;\n}\n\n/// Registry holding all curve implementations.\n#[derive(Default, Clone)]\nstruct CurveRegistry {\n    curves: Arc<RwLock<Vec<Box<dyn SigningCurve>>>>,\n}\n\nimpl CurveRegistry {\n    fn register(&mut self, curve: Box<dyn SigningCurve>) {\n        tokio::task::block_in_place(|| {\n            let mut guard = futures::executor::block_on(self.curves.write());\n            guard.push(curve);\n        });\n    }\n\n    fn get(&self, algo: CryptoAlgorithm) -> Option<&dyn SigningCurve> {\n        tokio::task::block_in_place(|| {\n            let guard = futures::executor::block_on(self.curves.read());\n            guard\n                .iter()\n                .find(|c| c.algorithm() ==   use ed25519_dalek::{Keypair, SignatureError, Signer};\n        let kp: Keypair = Keypair::generate(&mut rand_core::OsRng);\n        Ok((kp.secret.to_bytes().to_vec(), kp.public.to_bytes().to_vec()))\n    }\n\n    fn sign(&self, secret: &[u8], message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError> {\n        use ed25519_dalek::{Keypair, PublicKey, SecretKey, Signer};\n        let secret = SecretKey::from_bytes(secret)?;\n        let public: PublicKey = (&secret).into();\n        let kp = Keypair { secret, public };\n        Ok(kp.sign(message).to_bytes().to_vec())\n    }\n\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        use ed25519_dalek::{PublicKey, Signature, Verifier};\n        let pk = PublicKey::from_bytes(public)?;\n        let sig = Signature::from_bytes(signature)?;\n        pk.verify(message, &sig)?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── Secp256k1 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Secp256k1Curve;\n\nimpl SigningCurve for Secp256k1Curve {\n    fn algorithm(&self) -> sk_bytes = ECSecret::from(sk).to_bytes().to_vec();\n        Ok((sk_bytes, pk.to_encoded_point(false).as_bytes().to_vec()))\n    }\n\n    fn sign(&self, secret: &[u8], message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError> {\n        use k256::ecdsa::{signature::Signer as _, Signature, SigningKey};\n        let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(secret.into())?;\n        let sig: Signature = sk.sign(message);\n        Ok(sig.as_ref().to_vec())\n    }\n\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        use k256::ecdsa::{signature::Verifier as _, Signature, VerifyingKey};\n        let vk = VerifyingKey::from_sec1_bytes(public)?;\n        let sig = Signature::from_der(signature)?;\n        vk.verify(message, &sig)?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Keystore (file-based JSON)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[async_trait]\ntrait KeyStore: Send + Sync {\n    async fn put(&self, wallet: &WalletMeta) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError>;\n    async fn get(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<WalletMeta>, KeyManagerError>;\n}\n\n/// A very small file-based keystore.\n///\n/// Thread-safe via `RwLock` plus atomic write-to-tmp-then-rename.\nstruct FileKeyStore {\n    path:   PathBuf,\n    cache:  RwLock<Vec<WalletMeta>>,\n}\n\nimpl FileKeyStore {\n    async fn open<P: AsRef<Path>>(p: P) -> Result<Self, KeyManagerError> {\n        let path = p.as_ref().to_path_buf();\n        if !path.exists() {\n            // create empty file\n            tokio::fs::write(&path, b\"[]\").await?;\n        }\n\n        let data = tokio::fs::read(&path).await?;\n        let wallets: Vec<WalletMeta> = serde_json::from_slice(&data)?;\n        Ok(Self {\n            path,\n            cache: RwLock::new(wallets),\n        })\n    }\n\n    async fn persist(&self, list: &[WalletMeta]) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        let tmp_path = self\n            .path\n            .with_extension(\"tmp\");\n\n tmp_file.sync_all()?;\n                fs::rename(&tmp_path, &self.path)?;\n                Ok(())\n            }\n        })\n        .await??;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl KeyStore for FileKeyStore {\n    async fn put(&self, wallet: &WalletMeta) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        let mut guard = self.cache.write().await;\n        guard.push(wallet.clone());\n        self.persist(&guard).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn get(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<WalletMeta>, KeyManagerError> {\n        let guard = self.cache.read().await;\n        Ok(guard.iter().cloned().find(|w| w.id == id))\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Crypto helpers (Argon2 + ChaCha20-Poly1305)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst CHACHA_NONCE_SIZE: usize = 12;\nconst ARGON_SALT_LEN: usize = 16;\nconst ARGON_MEM_COST_KIB: u32 = 64 * 1024; // 64 MiB\nconst ARGON_ITER: u32 = 3;\n\nfn encrypt_secret(\n    password: &[u8],\n    secret: &[u8],\n) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, [u8; ARGON_SALT_LEN], [u8; CHACHA_NONCE_SIZE]), KeyManagerError> {\n    let = ChaCha20Poly1305::new(Key::from_slice(&key));\n    let plaintext = cipher.decrypt(Nonce::from_slice(nonce), ciphertext)?;\n    Ok(plaintext)\n}\n\nfn derive_key(password: &[u8], salt: &[u8]) -> ARGON_MEM_COST_KIB,\n        ARGON_ITER,\n        1,\n        Some(32),\n    )?;\n    let argon = Argon2::new(argon2::Algorithm::Argon2id, Version::V0x13, params);\n    let hash = PartialEq, Eq)]\n   107→pub enum ProposalOutcome {\n   108→    Accepted,\n   109→    Rejected,\n   110→    QuorumNotMet,\n   111→}\n   112→\n   113→/// Storage abstraction – can be backed by Postgres, 117→\n   118→    async fn load_proposal(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Proposal, ProposalError>;\n   119→\n   120→    async fn save_vote(\n   121→        &self,\n   122→        proposal_id: Uuid,\n   123→  154→\n   155→/// A default in-memory storage for local testing.\n   156→///\n   157→/// THIS IS NOT SUITABLE FOR PRODUCTION but helps keeping the code\n   158→/// self-contained for demonstration       self.proposals\n   176→            .read()\n   177→            .await\n   178→            .get(&id)\n   179→            .cloned()\n   180→            .ok_or(ProposalError::NotFound(id))\n   181→    }\n   182→\n   183→    async fn save_vote(\n   184→        &self,\n   185→        proposal_id: Uuid,\n   186→        voter: &str,\n   187→        choice: VoteChoice,\n   188→    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n   189→        let mut map = self.proposals.write().await;\n   190→        let proposal = map\n   191→            .get_mut(&proposal_id)\n   192→            .ok_or(ProposalError::NotFound(proposal_id))?;\n   193→\n   194→        if proposal.status != ProposalStatus::Active {\n   195→            return Err(ProposalError::Finalized);\n   196→        }\n   197→        if proposal.votes.contains_key(voter) {\n   198→            return Err(ProposalError::DuplicateVote);\n   199→        }\n   200→\n   201→        proposal.votes.insert(voter.to_owned(), .collect())\n   214→    }\n   215→\n   216→    async fn finalize(\n   217→        &self,\n   218→        proposal_id: Uuid,\n   219→        _outcome: ProposalOutcome,\n   220→    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n   221→        let mut map = self.proposals.write().await;\n   222→        let proposal = map\n   223→            .get_mut(&proposal_id)\n   224→            .ok_or(ProposalError::NotFound(proposal_id))?;\n   225→        proposal.status = ProposalStatus::Finalized;\n   226→        Ok(())\n   227→    }\n   228→}\n   229→\n   230→/// A no-op event publisher, {\n   236→        // swallow\n   237→    }\n   238→}\n   239→\n   240→/// The main façade exposed to the outer world.  All methods are\n   241→/// thread-safe and async aware.\n   242→pub struct ProposalEngine<S, E, V>\n   243→where\n   244→    S: ProposalStore,\n   245→    E: EventPublisher,\n   246→    V: SignatureVerifier,\n   247→{\n   248→    store: Arc<S>,\n   249→    events: Arc<E>,\n   250→    verifier: Arc<V>,\n   251→    config: GovernanceParameters,\n   252→    /// Task handle for the background finalizer loop.\n   253→    finalizer_task: RwLock<Option<JoinHandle<()>>>,\n   254→}\n   255→\n   256→impl<S, E, V> ProposalEngine<S, E, V>\n   257→where\n   258→    S: ProposalStore,\n   259→    E: EventPublisher,\n   260→    V: SignatureVerifier,\n   261→{\n   262→    pub fn new(store: Arc<S>, events: Arc<E>, verifier: Arc<V>) -> Self {\n   263→        let config = GovernanceParameters {\n   264→            voting_period: Duration::from_secs(60 * 60 * 24), // 24h\n   265→            quorum_pct: 15,\n   266→            pass_threshold_pct: 50,\n   267→        };\n   268→\n   269→        Self {\n   270→            store,\n   271→            events,\n   272→            verifier,\n   273→            config,\n   274→            finalizer_task: RwLock::new(None),\n   275→        }\n   276→    }\n   277→\n   278→    /// Spawns the periodic finalization loop.  Calling this twice is a\n   279→    /// no-op.\n   280→    pub async fn start(&self) {\n   281→        let mut guard = self.finalizer_task.write().await;\n   282→        if guard.is_some() {\n   283→            return;\n   284→        }\n   285→        let store = Arc::clone(&self.store);\n   286→        let events = Arc::clone(&self.events);\n   287→        let params = self.config.clone();\n   288→\n   289→        *guard = Some(tokio::spawn(async move {\n   290→            let mut ticker = time::interval(Duration::from_secs(30));\n   291→            loop {\n   292→                ticker.tick().await;\n   293→                if let Err(err) = finalize_expired(&store, &events, &params).await {\n   294→                    error!(\"auto-finalization cycle failed: {err:?}\");\n   295→                }\n   296→            }\n   297→        }));\n   298→    }\n   299→\n   300→    /// Creates and stores a new proposal.  The caller is responsible for\n   301→    /// signing the payload.\n   302→    ///\n   303→    /// `signature` must verify against `author` and the payload bytes\n   304→    /// (as canonical JSON).\n   305→    pub async fn create_proposal(\n   306→        &self,\n   307→        author: &str,\n   308→        payload: serde_json::Value,\n   309→        signature: &[u8],\n   310→    ) -> Result<Uuid, ProposalError> {\n   311→        let json_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&payload)\n   312→            .map_err(|e| ProposalError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n   313→\n   314→        self.verifier\n   315→            .verify(author, &json_bytes, signature)\n   316→            .await?;\n   317→\n   318→        let now = Utc::now();\n   319→        let expires_at = now + chrono::Duration::from_std(self.config.voting_period)\n   320→            .map_err(|e| ProposalError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n   321→\n   322→        let proposal = Proposal {\n   323→            id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n   324→            author: author.to_owned(),\n   325→            status: ProposalStatus::Active,\n   326→            created_at: now,\n   327→            expires_at,\n   328→            payload,\n   329→            votes: HashMap::default(),\n   330→        };\n   331→\n   332→        self.store.save_proposal(&proposal).await?;\n   333→        self.events\n   334→            .publish(GovernanceEvent::ProposalCreated {\n   335→                proposal_id: proposal.id,\n   336→                author: author.to_owned(),\n   337→            })\n   338→            .await;\n   339→\n   340→        Ok(proposal.id)\n   341→    }\n   342→\n   343→    /// Cast a vote on an active proposal.\n   344→    pub async fn cast_vote(\n   345→        &self,\n   346→        proposal_id: Uuid,\n   347→        voter: &str,\n   348→        choice: VoteChoice,\n   349→        signature: &[u8],\n   350→    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n   351→        // Message to sign = proposal_id (16 bytes) || choice (1 byte)\n   352→        let mut msg = proposal_id.as_bytes().to_vec();\n   353→        msg.push(choice as u8);\n   354→\n   355→        self.verifier.verify(voter, &msg, signature).await?;\n   356→\n   357→        self.store\n   358→            .save_vote(proposal_id, voter, choice)\n   359→            .await\n   360→            .map_err(|e| {\n   361→                debug!(\"save_vote failed: {e}\");\n   362→                e\n   363→            })?;\n   364→\n   365→        self.events\n   366→            .publish(GovernanceEvent::VoteCast {\n   367→                proposal_id,\n   368→                voter: voter.to_owned(),\n   369→                choice,\n   370→            })\n   371→            .await;\n   372→\n   373→        Ok(())\n   374→    }\n   375→\n   376→    /// Forces finalization of an active proposal, ignoring `expires_at`\n   377→    /// (useful for emergency admin operations).\n   378→    pub async fn finalize_now(&self, proposal_id: Uuid) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n   379→        let proposal = self.store.load_proposal(proposal_id).await?;\n   380→        let outcome = compute_outcome(&proposal, &self.config);\n   381→        self.store.finalize(proposal_id, outcome).await?;\n   382→        self.events\n   383→            .publish(GovernanceEvent::ProposalFinalized { proposal_id, result: outcome })\n   384→            .await;\n   385→        Ok(())\n   386→    }\n   387→}\n   388→\n   389→/// Computes the outcome according to yes/no/abstain counts and the\n   390→/// engine’s governance parameters.\n   391→fn compute_outcome(proposal: &Proposal, params: &GovernanceParameters) -> ProposalOutcome {\n   392→    let total_votes = proposal.votes.len() as u64;\n   393→    if total_votes == 0 {\n   394→        return ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n   395→    }\n   396→\n   397→    let yes = proposal\n   398→        .votes\n   399→        .values()\n   400→        .filter(|&&c| c == VoteChoice::Yes)\n   401→        .count() as u64;\n   402→    let no = proposal\n   403→        .votes\n   404→        .values()\n   405→        .filter(|&&c| c == VoteChoice::No)\n   406→        .count() as u64;\n   407→    let abstain = total_votes - yes - no;\n   408→\n   409→    debug!(\n   410→        \"Computing outcome – yes:{yes}, no:{no}, abstain:{abstain}, total:{total_votes}\"\n   411→    );\n   412→\n   413→    let quorum_met = (total_votes * 100 / u64::MAX.max(total_votes)) >= params.quorum_pct as u64;\n   414→\n   415→    if !quorum_met {\n   416→        return ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n   417→    }\n   418→\n   419→    let yes_pct = yes * 100 / total_votes;\n   420→    if yes_pct >= params.pass_threshold_pct as u64 {\n   421→        ProposalOutcome::Accepted\n   422→    } else {\n   423→        ProposalOutcome::Rejected\n   424→    }\n   425→}\n   426→\n   427→/// Periodic task for auto-finalizing expired proposals.\n   428→async fn finalize_expired<S: ProposalStore, E: EventPublisher>(\n   429→    store: &Arc<S>,\n   430→    events: &Arc<E>,\n   431→    params: &GovernanceParameters,\n   432→) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n   433→    let now = Utc::now();\n   434→ Proposal Engine\n//!\n//! This module owns the lifecycle of a governance proposal—from\n//! creation to finalization—inside the CanvasChain Symphony governance\n//! micro-service.  It is intentionally decoupled from concrete storage,\n//! crypto or transport layers via small, test-friendly traits so that\n//! the engine can be reused inside CLI tools, HTTP controllers or gRPC\n//! handlers without recompilation.\n\n#![allow(clippy::module_name_repetitions)]\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::{HashMap, HashSet},\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::Duration,\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse log::{debug, error, info, warn};\nuse rand::Rng;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::{\n    sync::RwLock,\n    task::JoinHandle,\n    time::{self, Instant},\n};\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\n/// Error type returned by the proposal engine.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ProposalError {\n    #[error(\"storage error: {0}\")]\n    Storage(String),\n    #[error(\"signature verification failed\")]\n    InvalidSignature,\n    #[error(\"proposal not found: {0}\")]\n    NotFound(Uuid),\n    #[error(\"vote already cast by voter\")]\n    DuplicateVote,\n    #[error(\"proposal already finalized\")]\n    Finalized,\n    #[error(\"internal error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\n/// Supported vote choices.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum VoteChoice {\n    Yes,\n    No,\n    Abstain,\n}\n\n/// Current status of a proposal.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum ProposalStatus {\n    Pending,\n    Active,\n    Finalized,\n}\n\n/// A governance proposal.  The semantics of the payload are opaque to\n/// the proposal engine and can encode anything from smart-contract\n/// upgrades to art-movement parameters.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Proposal {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub author: String,\n    pub status: ProposalStatus,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub expires_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub payload: serde_json::Value,\n    pub votes: HashMap<String, VoteChoice>,\n}\n\n/// Governance parameters under which a proposal is evaluated.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\npub struct GovernanceParameters {\n    /// Minimum voting period.\n    pub voting_period: Duration,\n    /// Quorum required as percentage (0–100).\n    pub quorum_pct: u8,\n    /// Minimum “Yes” ratio required to pass (0–100).\n    pub pass_threshold_pct: u8,\n}\n\n/// Event emitted by the proposal engine to the wider event-bus.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum GovernanceEvent {\n    ProposalCreated { proposal_id: Uuid, author: String },\n    VoteCast {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: String,\n        choice: VoteChoice,\n    },\n    ProposalFinalized {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        result: ProposalOutcome,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Finalization result.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum ProposalOutcome {\n    Accepted,\n    Rejected,\n    QuorumNotMet,\n}\n\n/// Storage abstraction – can be backed by Postgres, RocksDB, Redis, etc.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait ProposalStore: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn save_proposal(&self, proposal: &Proposal) -> Result<(), ProposalError>;\n\n    async fn load_proposal(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Proposal, ProposalError>;\n\n    async fn save_vote(\n        &self,\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: &str,\n        choice: VoteChoice,\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError>;\n\n    async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Proposal>, ProposalError>;\n\n    async fn finalize(\n        &self,\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        outcome: ProposalOutcome,\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError>;\n}\n\n/// Event publisher abstraction – decouples the engine from concrete\n/// transport: NATS, Kafka, in-process channel, etc.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn publish(&self, event: GovernanceEvent);\n}\n\n/// Signature verification strategy.  Each wallet/curve implementation\n/// can plug its own verifier (Ed25519, BLS, post-quantum…).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait SignatureVerifier: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn verify(\n        &self,\n        wallet_address: &str,\n        payload: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError>;\n}\n\n/// A default in-memory storage for local testing.\n///\n/// THIS IS NOT SUITABLE FOR PRODUCTION but helps keeping the code\n/// self-contained for demonstration purposes.\n#[derive(Default)]\npub struct InMemoryStore {\n    proposals: RwLock<HashMap<Uuid, Proposal>>,\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl ProposalStore for InMemoryStore {\n    async fn save_proposal(&self, proposal: &Proposal) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        self.proposals\n            .write()\n            .await\n            .insert(proposal.id, proposal.clone());\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn load_proposal(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Proposal, ProposalError> {\n        self.proposals\n            .read()\n            .await\n            .get(&id)\n            .cloned()\n            .ok_or(ProposalError::NotFound(id))\n    }\n\n    async fn save_vote(\n        &self,\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: &str,\n        choice: VoteChoice,\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        let mut map = self.proposals.write().await;\n        let proposal = map\n            .get_mut(&proposal_id)\n            .ok_or(ProposalError::NotFound(proposal_id))?;\n\n        if proposal.status != ProposalStatus::Active {\n            return Err(ProposalError::Finalized);\n        }\n        if proposal.votes.contains_key(voter) {\n            return Err(ProposalError::DuplicateVote);\n        }\n\n        proposal.votes.insert(voter.to_owned(), choice);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Proposal>, ProposalError> {\n        Ok(self\n            .proposals\n            .read()\n            .await\n            .values()\n            .filter(|p| p.status == ProposalStatus::Active)\n            .cloned()\n            .collect())\n    }\n\n    async fn finalize(\n        &self,\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        _outcome: ProposalOutcome,\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        let mut map = self.proposals.write().await;\n        let proposal = map\n            .get_mut(&proposal_id)\n            .ok_or(ProposalError::NotFound(proposal_id))?;\n        proposal.status = ProposalStatus::Finalized;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// A no-op event publisher, }\n}\n\n/// The main façade exposed to the outer world.  All methods are\n/// thread-safe and async aware.\npub struct ProposalEngine<S, E, V>\nwhere\n    S: ProposalStore,\n    E: EventPublisher,\n    V: SignatureVerifier,\n{\n    store: Arc<S>,\n    events: Arc<E>,\n    verifier: Arc<V>,\n    config: GovernanceParameters,\n    /// Task handle for the background finalizer loop.\n    finalizer_task: RwLock<Option<JoinHandle<()>>>,\n}\n\nimpl<S, E, V> ProposalEngine<S, E, V>\nwhere\n    S: ProposalStore,\n    E: EventPublisher,\n    V: SignatureVerifier,\n{\n    pub fn new(store: Arc<S>, events: Arc<E>, verifier: Arc<V>) -> Self {\n        let config = GovernanceParameters {\n            voting_period: Duration::from_secs(60 * 60 * 24), // 24h\n            quorum_pct: 15,\n            pass_threshold_pct: 50,\n        };\n\n        Self {\n            store,\n            events,\n            verifier,\n            config,\n            finalizer_task: RwLock::new(None),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Spawns the periodic finalization loop.  Calling this twice is a\n    /// no-op.\n    pub async fn start(&self) {\n        let mut guard = self.finalizer_task.write().await;\n        if guard.is_some() {\n            return;\n        }\n        let store = Arc::clone(&self.store);\n        let events = Arc::clone(&self.events);\n        let params = self.config.clone();\n\n        *guard = Some(tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let mut ticker = time::interval(Duration::from_secs(30));\n            loop {\n                ticker.tick().await;\n                if let Err(err) = finalize_expired(&store, &events, &params).await {\n                    error!(\"auto-finalization cycle failed: {err:?}\");\n                }\n            }\n        }));\n    }\n\n    /// Creates and stores a new proposal.  The caller is responsible for\n    /// signing the payload.\n    ///\n    /// `signature` must verify against `author` and the payload bytes\n    /// (as canonical JSON).\n    pub async fn create_proposal(\n        &self,\n        author: &str,\n        payload: serde_json::Value,\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Uuid, ProposalError> {\n        let json_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&payload)\n            .map_err(|e| ProposalError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        self.verifier\n            .verify(author, &json_bytes, signature)\n            .await?;\n\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let expires_at = now + chrono::Duration::from_std(self.config.voting_period)\n            .map_err(|e| ProposalError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        let proposal = Proposal {\n            id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n            author: author.to_owned(),\n            status: ProposalStatus::Active,\n            created_at: now,\n            expires_at,\n            payload,\n            votes: HashMap::default(),\n        };\n\n        self.store.save_proposal(&proposal).await?;\n        self.events\n            .publish(GovernanceEvent::ProposalCreated {\n                proposal_id: proposal.id,\n                author: author.to_owned(),\n            })\n            .await;\n\n        Ok(proposal.id)\n    }\n\n    /// Cast a vote on an active proposal.\n    pub async fn cast_vote(\n        &self,\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: &str,\n        choice: VoteChoice,\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        // Message to sign = proposal_id (16 bytes) || choice (1 byte)\n        let mut msg = proposal_id.as_bytes().to_vec();\n        msg.push(choice as u8);\n\n        self.verifier.verify(voter, &msg, signature).await?;\n\n        self.store\n            .save_vote(proposal_id, voter, choice)\n            .await\n            .map_err(|e| {\n                debug!(\"save_vote failed: {e}\");\n                e\n            })?;\n\n        self.events\n            .publish(GovernanceEvent::VoteCast {\n                proposal_id,\n                voter: voter.to_owned(),\n                choice,\n            })\n            .await;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Forces finalization of an active proposal, ignoring `expires_at`\n    /// (useful for emergency admin operations).\n    pub async fn finalize_now(&self, proposal_id: Uuid) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        let proposal = self.store.load_proposal(proposal_id).await?;\n        let outcome = compute_outcome(&proposal, &self.config);\n        self.store.finalize(proposal_id, outcome).await?;\n        self.events\n            .publish(GovernanceEvent::ProposalFinalized { proposal_id, result: outcome })\n            .await;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Computes the outcome according to yes/no/abstain counts and the\n/// engine’s governance parameters.\nfn compute_outcome(proposal: &Proposal, params: &GovernanceParameters) -> ProposalOutcome {\n    let total_votes = proposal.votes.len() as u64;\n    if total_votes == 0 {\n        return ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n    }\n\n    let yes = proposal\n        .votes\n        .values()\n        .filter(|&&c| c == VoteChoice::Yes)\n        .count() as u64;\n    let no = proposal\n        .votes\n        .values()\n        .filter(|&&c| c == VoteChoice::No)\n        .count() as u64;\n    let abstain = total_votes - yes - no;\n\n    debug!(\n        \"Computing outcome – yes:{yes}, no:{no}, abstain:{abstain}, total:{total_votes}\"\n    );\n\n    let quorum_met = (total_votes * 100 / u64::MAX.max(total_votes)) >= params.quorum_pct as u64;\n\n    if !quorum_met {\n        return ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n    }\n\n    let yes_pct = yes * 100 / total_votes;\n    if yes_pct >= params.pass_threshold_pct as u64 {\n        ProposalOutcome::Accepted\n    } else {\n        ProposalOutcome::Rejected\n    }\n}\n\n/// Periodic task for auto-finalizing expired proposals.\nasync fn finalize_expired<S: ProposalStore, E: EventPublisher>(\n    store: &Arc<S>,\n    events: &Arc<E>,\n    params: &GovernanceParameters,\n) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n    let now = Utc::now();\n    for mut proposal in store.list_active().await? {\n        if proposal.expires_at <= now {\n            let outcome = compute_outcome(&proposal, params);\n            store.finalize(proposal.id, outcome).await?;\n            events\n                .publish(GovernanceEvent::ProposalFinalized {\n                    proposal_id: proposal.id,\n                    result: outcome,\n                })\n                .await;\n            info!(\"proposal {} finalized with result {:?}\", proposal.id, outcome);\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// A mock signature verifier that accepts 1% of the messages randomly\n/// as invalid.  Replace with a real crypto verifier in production.\npub struct PseudoVerifier;\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl SignatureVerifier for PseudoVerifier {\n    async fn verify(\n        &self,\n        _wallet_address: &str,\n        _payload: &[u8],\n        _signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();\n        let rejected: bool = rng.gen_bool(0.01);\n        if rejected {\n            Err(ProposalError::InvalidSignature)\n        } else {\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use serde_json::json;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn happy_path() {\n        let engine = ProposalEngine::new(\n            Arc::new(InMemoryStore::default()),\n            Arc::new(NullPublisher),\n            Arc::new(PseudoVerifier),\n        );\n\n        let payload = json!({\n            \"type\": \"change_quorum\",\n            \"new_quorum\": 20\n        });\n        let author = \"wallet_1\";\n\n        let proposal_id = engine\n            .create_proposal(author, payload, &[0u8; 64])\n            .await\n            .expect(\"create proposal\");\n\n        engine\n            .cast_vote(proposal_id, \"voter_a\", VoteChoice::Yes, &[0u8; 64])\n            .await\n            .expect(\"vote a\");\n\n        engine\n            .cast_vote(proposal_id, \"voter_b\", VoteChoice::Yes, &[0u8; 64])\n            .await\n            .expect(\"vote b\");\n\n        engine.finalize_now(proposal_id).await.expect(\"finalize\");\n\n        let proposal = engine\n            .store\n            .load_proposal(proposal_id)\n            .await\n            .expect(\"load final\");\n        assert_eq!(proposal.status, ProposalStatus::Finalized);\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":516,"startLine":1,"totalLines":516}}}

This module exposes a small, but representative, subset of the public HTTP\n//! interface that external clients (CLI, mobile wallets, browsers or embedded\n//! installations) use to interact with the distributed CanvasChain back-end.\n//!\n//! Whenever a request arrives, we\n//! perform cheap syntactic validation, enrich the tracing span with relevant\n//! metadata and forward the call downstream.  Results are converted back to\n//! idiomatic JSON before being returned to the caller.\n//!\n//! ┌──────────────────────────┐      gRPC       ┌──────────────────────────┐\n//! │      HTTP  Clients       ├────────────────►│  CanvasChain Services    │\n//! └──────────────────────────┘                 └──────────────────────────┘\n//!\n//! \"status\": status })))\n}\n\n#[instrument(name = \"mint_nft\", skip(state, headers, payload))]\nasync fn mint_nft(\n    State(state): State<AppState>,\n    headers: HeaderMap,\n    Json(payload): Json<MintRequest>,\n) -> Result<impl IntoResponse, ApiError> {\n    // Example of cheap *syntactic* validation – expensive checks are left for\n    // the Minting service.\n    if payload.metadata_uri.is_empty() {\n        return Err(ApiError::BadRequest(\"metadata_uri must not be empty\".into()));\n    }\n\n    // Correlate downstream span with the inbound request\n    let span = Span::current();\n    span.record(\"artist_id\", &payload.artist_id.as_str());\n\n    let grpc_req = tonic::Request::new(MintNftRequest {\n        nft_id: Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),\n }),\n    ))\n}\n\n#[instrument(name = \"get_nft\", skip(state))]\nasync fn get_nft(\n    State(state): State<AppState>,\n    Path(nft_id): Path<String>,\n) -> Result<Json<Nft>, ApiError> {\n    let nft.nft_id.is_empty() {\n        return Err(ApiError::NotFound(format!(\"NFT {nft_id} does not exist\")));\n    }\n\n    Ok(Json(Nft {\n        nft_id: nft.nft_id,\n        owner: nft.owner,\n        metadata_uri: nft.metadata_uri,\n        ask_price: if nft.ask_price.is_empty() { None } else { Some(nft.ask_price) },\n        created_at: nft.created_at,\n    }))\n}\n\n#[instrument(name = \"list_nfts\", skip(state))]\nasync fn list_nfts(\n    State(state): State<AppState>,\n    Query(pg): Query<Pagination>,\n) -> Result<Json<Paginated<Nft>>, ApiError> {\n    // The `list_nfts` RPC accepts offset/limit rather than page/per_page.\n    let offset            = (pg.page.saturating_sub(1) * pg.per_page) as u64;\n    let grpc_req          = tonic::Request::new(proto::minting::ListNftsRequest {\n        offset,\n        limit: pg.per_page as u64,\n    });\n    let list_resp         = state.minting.clone().list_nfts(grpc_req).await?.into_inner();\n    let total             = list_resp.total as u32;\n    let data: Vec<Nft>    = list_resp\n        .nfts\n        .into_iter()\n        .map(|n| Nft {\n            nft_id: n.nft_id,\n            owner: n.owner,\n            metadata_uri: n.metadata_uri,\n            ask_price: if n.ask_price.is_empty() { None } else { Some(n.ask_price) },\n            created_at: n.created_at,\n        })\n        .collect();\n\n    Ok(Json(Paginated {\n        data,\n        page: pg.page,\n        per_page: pg.per_page,\n        total,\n    }))\n}\n\n#[instrument(name = \"cast_vote\", skip(state, payload))]\nasync fn cast_vote(\n    State(state): State<AppState>,\n    Json(payload): Json<VoteRequest>,\n) -> Result<Json<VoteResponse>, ApiError> {\n    if ![\"yes\", \"no\", \"abstain\"].contains(&payload.choice.to_lowercase().as_str()) {\n        return Err(ApiError::BadRequest(\"choice must be Yes|No|Abstain\".into()));\n    }\n\n    let grpc_req = tonic::Request::new(proto::governance::CastVoteRequest {\n        proposal_id: payload.proposal_id,\n        voter: payload.voter,\n        choice: payload.choice,\n    });\n\n    let resp = state\n        .governance\n        .clone()\n        .cast_vote(grpc_req)\n        .await?\n        .into_inner();\n\n    Ok(Json(VoteResponse { accepted: resp.accepted }))\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Graceful bootstrap entry-point (for local development)\n// Kubernetes Rust operator, so the\n/// binary entry-point lives elsewhere.  This helper is provided purely for\n/// convenience during local tinkering (`cargo run -p api_gateway`).\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() -> Result<(), anyhow::Error> {\n    tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();\n\n    let addr: SocketAddr = std::env::var(\"BIND_ADDR\")\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"127.0.0.1:8080\".into())\n        .parse()?;\n\n    // Connect to the internal service mesh gateway.\n    let grpc_ep = std::env::var(\"GRPC_ENDPOINT\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \"http://127.0.0.1:50051\".into());\n    let state   = AppState::connect_all(grpc_ep).await?;\n\n    info!(\"🚀  API-Gateway listening on {addr}\");\n    axum::Server::bind(&addr)\n        .serve(router(state).into_make_service())\n        .await?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n```","numLines":456,"startLine":1,"totalLines":456}}}

Persistent world-state database abstraction for CanvasChain Symphony.\n     3→//!\n     4→//! The `StateDb` is the canonical source of truth for chain execution.  It\n     5→//! stores **accounts**, **contract byte-code** and **key–value storage** for\n     6→//! every smart contract.  Each block commit generates a deterministic *state\n     7→//! root* (a Blake3 Merkle accumulation of all key–value pairs) that can later\n     8→//! be proven against light-client requests or off-chain indexers.\n     9→//!\n    10→//! Elasticity requirements of the Symphony runtime dictate that the database\n    11→//! must support:\n    12→//!   * atomic multi-key updates (Write-Set)\n    13→//!   * cheap, read-only snapshots per block height\n    14→//!   * forward-only pruning (retain the last *N* roots and fast-prune the rest)\n    15→//!\n    16→//! This implementation relies on `sled` for embedded, high-performance storage\n    17→//! and an append-only **versioned column family** layout to avoid write\n    18→//! amplification while retaining historical states.\n    19→\n    20→use std::{\n    21→    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    22→    sync::{\n    23→        atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},\n    24→        Arc,\n    25→    },\n    26→};\n numerical height in big-endian to preserve lexicographic order.\n    50→fn compose_versioned_key(height: u64, raw_key: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    51→    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(8 + raw_key.len());\n    52→    out.extend_from_slice(&height.to_be_bytes());\n    53→    out.extend_from_slice(raw_key);\n    54→    out\n    55→}\n    56→\n    57→/// Decode a versioned key returning u64::from_be_bytes(height_bytes.try_into().expect(\"8 bytes\")),\n    62→        raw,\n    63→    )\n    64→}\n    65→\n    66→/// High-level account record stored in the `StateDb`.\n    67→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\n    68→pub struct AccountState {\n    69→    pub balance: u128,\n    70→    pub nonce: u64,\n    71→    /// Hash of contract bytecode storage_root: [0u8; 32],\n    84→        }\n    85→    }\n    86→}\n    87→\n    88→/// Arbitrary write operation encoded in a block's *write-set*.\n    89→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    90→pub enum WriteOp<V> {\n    91→    Put(Vec<u8>, V),\n    92→    Delete(Vec<u8>),\n    93→}\n    94→\n    95→/// Aggregated writes performed by the execution engine for a single StateCommitEvent {\n   101→    pub block_height: u64,\n   102→    pub new_state_root: StateRoot,\n   103→}\n   104→\n   105→/// Custom errors bubbled up by the `StateDb`.\n   106→#[derive(Debug, Error)]\n   107→pub enum StateDbError {\n   108→    #[error(\"sled error: {0}\")]\n   109→    Sled(#[from] sled::Error),\n   110→\n   111→    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n   112→    Bincode(#[from] bincode::Error),\n   113→\n   114→    #[error(\"conflicting write transaction\")]\n   115→    Conflict,\n   116→\n   117→    #[error(\"requested historical height ({requested}) is pruned – oldest available: {oldest}\")]\n   118→    Pruned { requested: u64, oldest: u64 },\n   119→}\n   120→\n   121→/// Primary database wrapper offering high-level typed APIs.\n   122→pub struct StateDb {\n   123→    db: Db,\n   124→    /// Tree for versioned state entries.\n   125→    tree: sled::Tree,\n   126→    /// Monotonically increasing block height.\n   127→    current_height:
