Arc<RwLock<()>>,\n   136→}\n   137→\n   138→impl StateDb {\n   139→    /// Open or create a new `StateDb` at `path`.\n   140→    pub fn open<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, StateDbError> {\n   141→        let db = sled::open(&path)?;\n   142→        let tree = db.open_tree(\"state\")?;\n   143→\n   144→        // Determine the current and oldest heights by scanning prefix keys.\n   145→        let (mut current_height, mut oldest_height) = (0u64, u64::MAX);\n   146→        for key in tree.iter().keys() {\n   147→            let k = key?;\n   148→            let (h, _) = split_versioned_key(&k);\n   149→            if h > current_height {\n   150→                current_height = h;\n   151→            }\n   152→            if h < oldest_height {\n   153→ .map(|ivec| bincode::deserialize(&ivec))\n   203→            .transpose()?)\n   204→    }\n   205→\n   206→    /// Store a single value at the *next* block height.\n   207→    ///\n   208→    /// This )]))\n   215→        .map(|_| ())\n   216→    }\n   217→\n   218→    /// Atomically apply a `write_set`, advance `current_height` and\n   219→    /// deterministically compute the new `StateRoot`.\n   220→    pub fn commit_block<V: Serialize>(&self, write_set: WriteSet<V>) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n   221→        let new_height = self.current_height.load(Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;\n   222→        {\n   223→            // Acquire read-lock to forbid pruning during commit.\n   224→            let _r = self.guard.read();\n   225→            // --- transactional write ---\n   226→            let result: TransactionResult<(), StateDbError, _> =\n   227→                (&self.tree).transaction(|tx_tree| {\n   228→                    for op in &write_set {\n   229→ ConflictableTransactionError::Abort(StateDbError::Sled(e)))?;\n   240→                            }\n   241→                        }\n   242→                    }\n   243→                    Ok(())\n   244→                });\n   245→\n   246→            match result {\n   247→                Ok(_) => {}\n   248→                Err(TransactionError::Storage(e)) => return Err(StateDbError::Sled(e)),\n   249→                Err(TransactionError::Abort(e)) => return Err(e),\n   250→            }\n   251→        }\n   252→\n   253→        // Sync to disk.\n   254→        self.db.flush()?;\n   255→\n   256→        // Re-compute and persist new root.\n   257→        let new_root = self.compute_state_root(new_height)?;\n   258→        self.current_height.store(new_height, Ordering::SeqCst);\n   259→\n   260→        // Notify subscribers – ignore send failures (no active listeners).\n   261→        let _ = self\n   262→            .event_tx\n   263→            .send(StateCommitEvent { block_height: new_height, new_state_root: new_root });\n   264→\n   265→        Ok(new_root)\n   266→    }\n   267→\n   268→    /// Deterministically compute the Blake3 root for a given `height`.\n   269→    ///\n   270→    /// The root is the hash of the concatenation of `hash(key) || hash(value)`\n   271→    /// in lexicographic key order.  While *not* a Patricia-Merkle tree, this\n   272→    /// approach provides collision resistance and is fast to compute.\n   273→    pub fn compute_state_root(&self, height: u64) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n   274→        let prefix = height.to_be_bytes();\n   275→        let mut hasher = Hasher::new();\n   276→\n   277→        for kv in self.tree.scan_prefix(prefix) {\n   278→            let (k, v) = kv?;\n   279→            hasher.update(blake3::hash(&k).as_bytes());\n   280→            hasher.update(blake3::hash(&v).as_bytes());\n   281→        }\n   282→        Ok(*hasher.finalize().as_bytes())\n   283→    }\n   284→\n   285→    /// Permanently remove historical states below `keep_from_height`.\n   286→    pub fn prune(&self, keep_from_height: u64) -> Persistent world-state database abstraction for CanvasChain Symphony.\n//!\n//! The `StateDb` is the canonical source of truth for chain execution.  It\n//! stores **accounts**, **contract byte-code** and **key–value storage** for\n//! every smart contract.  Each block commit generates a deterministic *state\n//! root* (a Blake3 Merkle accumulation of all key–value pairs) that can later\n//! be proven against light-client requests or off-chain indexers.\n//!\n//! Elasticity requirements of the Symphony runtime dictate that the database\n//! must support:\n//!   * atomic multi-key updates (Write-Set)\n//!   * cheap, read-only snapshots per block height\n//!   * forward-only pruning (retain the last *N* roots and fast-prune the rest)\n//!\n//! This implementation relies on `sled` for embedded, high-performance storage\n//! and an append-only **versioned column family** layout to avoid write\n//! amplification while retaining historical states.\n\nuse std::{\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    sync::{\n        atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},\n        Arc,\n    },\n};\n\nuse blake3::Hasher;\nuse parking_lot::RwLock;\nuse serde::{de::DeserializeOwned, Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse sled::{\n    transaction::{\n        ConflictableTransactionError, TransactionError, TransactionResult, Transactional,\n        TransactionalTree,\n    },\n    Db, IVec,\n};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::sync::broadcast;\n\n/// Logical identifier for an account or contract.\npub type Address = [u8; 32];\n\n/// Hashed representation of the entire world state for a given block height.\npub type StateRoot = [u8; 32];\n\n/// Ordering key prefix for versioned writes: `{height}/{raw_key}`.\n///\n/// We keep the numerical height in big-endian to preserve lexicographic order.\nfn compose_versioned_key(height: u64, raw_key: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(8 + raw_key.len());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&height.to_be_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(raw_key);\n    out\n}\n\n/// Decode a versioned key returning `(height, raw_key)`.\nfn split_versioned_key(versioned: &[u8]) -> (u64, &[u8]) {\n    let (height_bytes, raw) = versioned.split_at(8);\n    (\n        u64::from_be_bytes(height_bytes.try_into().expect(\"8 bytes\")),\n        raw,\n    )\n}\n\n/// High-level account record stored in the `StateDb`.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct AccountState {\n    pub balance: u128,\n    pub nonce: u64,\n    /// Hash of contract bytecode (if any).\n    pub code_hash: Option<[u8; 32]>,\n    /// Merklized root of the contract storage tree.\n    pub storage_root: [u8; 32],\n}\n\nimpl Default for AccountState {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            balance: 0,\n            nonce: 0,\n            code_hash: None,\n            storage_root: [0u8; 32],\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Arbitrary write operation encoded in a block's *write-set*.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum WriteOp<V> {\n    Put(Vec<u8>, V),\n    Delete(Vec<u8>),\n}\n\n/// Aggregated writes performed by the execution engine for a single block.\npub type WriteSet<V> = Vec<WriteOp<V>>;\n\n/// Notification emitted after every successful `commit_block`.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct StateCommitEvent {\n    pub block_height: u64,\n    pub new_state_root: StateRoot,\n}\n\n/// Custom errors bubbled up by the `StateDb`.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum StateDbError {\n    #[error(\"sled error: {0}\")]\n    Sled(#[from] sled::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Bincode(#[from] bincode::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"conflicting write transaction\")]\n    Conflict,\n\n    #[error(\"requested historical height ({requested}) is pruned – oldest available: {oldest}\")]\n    Pruned { requested: u64, oldest: u64 },\n}\n\n/// Primary database wrapper offering high-level typed APIs.\npub struct StateDb {\n    db: Db,\n    /// Tree for versioned state entries.\n    tree: sled::Tree,\n    /// Monotonically increasing block height.\n    current_height: AtomicU64,\n    /// Oldest height still retained (everything below is pruned).\n    oldest_height: AtomicU64,\n    /// Broadcast channel for commit notifications.\n    event_tx: broadcast::Sender<StateCommitEvent>,\n    /// Path on disk (used on `prune`).\n    _path: PathBuf,\n    /// Global rw-lock for snapshot safety.\n    guard: Arc<RwLock<()>>,\n}\n\nimpl StateDb {\n    /// Open or create a new `StateDb` at `path`.\n    pub fn open<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, StateDbError> {\n        let db = sled::open(&path)?;\n        let tree = db.open_tree(\"state\")?;\n\n        // Determine the current and oldest heights by scanning prefix keys.\n        let (mut current_height, mut oldest_height) = (0u64, u64::MAX);\n        for key in tree.iter().keys() {\n            let k = key?;\n            let (h, _) = split_versioned_key(&k);\n            if h > current_height {\n                current_height = h;\n            }\n            if h < oldest_height {\n                oldest_height = h;\n            }\n        }\n        if oldest_height == u64::MAX {\n            oldest_height = 0;\n        }\n\n        let (event_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(64);\n        Ok(Self {\n            db,\n            tree,\n            current_height: AtomicU64::new(current_height),\n            oldest_height: AtomicU64::new(oldest_height),\n            event_tx,\n            _path: path.as_ref().to_path_buf(),\n            guard: Arc::new(RwLock::new(())),\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to state-commit events.\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<StateCommitEvent> {\n        self.event_tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    /// Retrieve the value associated with `raw_key` *at the latest height*.\n    pub fn get<V: DeserializeOwned>(&self, raw_key: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<V>, StateDbError> {\n        let height = self.current_height.load(Ordering::SeqCst);\n        self.get_at(height, raw_key)\n    }\n\n    /// Retrieve a historical value at `height`.\n    pub fn get_at<V: DeserializeOwned>(\n        &self,\n        height: u64,\n        raw_key: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Option<V>, StateDbError> {\n        let oldest = self.oldest_height.load(Ordering::SeqCst);\n        if height < oldest {\n            return Err(StateDbError::Pruned {\n                requested: height,\n                oldest,\n            });\n        }\n\n        let _r = self.guard.read(); // protect against concurrent pruning\n        let versioned_key = compose_versioned_key(height, raw_key);\n        Ok(self\n            .tree\n            .get(versioned_key)?\n            .map(|ivec| bincode::deserialize(&ivec))\n            .transpose()?)\n    }\n\n    /// Store a single value at the *next* block height.\n    ///\n    /// This is mostly useful for tests; production execution typically uses\n    /// `commit_block` that atomically writes a full `WriteSet`.\n    pub fn put<V: Serialize>(&self, raw_key: &[u8], value: &V) -> Result<(), StateDbError> {\n        self.commit_block::<V>(WriteSet::from([WriteOp::Put(\n            raw_key.to_vec(),\n            value.clone(),\n        )]))\n        .map(|_| ())\n    }\n\n    /// Atomically apply a `write_set`, advance `current_height` and\n    /// deterministically compute the new `StateRoot`.\n    pub fn commit_block<V: Serialize>(&self, write_set: WriteSet<V>) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n        let new_height = self.current_height.load(Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;\n        {\n            // Acquire read-lock to forbid pruning during commit.\n            let _r = self.guard.read();\n            // --- transactional write ---\n            let result: TransactionResult<(), StateDbError, _> =\n                (&self.tree).transaction(|tx_tree| {\n                    for op in &write_set {\n                        match op {\n                            WriteOp::Put(k, v) => {\n                                let vk = compose_versioned_key(new_height, k);\n                                let bytes = bincode::serialize(v)?;\n                                tx_tree.insert(vk, bytes)\n                                    .map_err(|e| ConflictableTransactionError::Abort(StateDbError::Sled(e)))?;\n                            }\n                            WriteOp::Delete(k) => {\n                                let vk = compose_versioned_key(new_height, k);\n                                tx_tree.remove(vk)\n                                    .map_err(|e| ConflictableTransactionError::Abort(StateDbError::Sled(e)))?;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                    Ok(())\n                });\n\n            match result {\n                Ok(_) => {}\n                Err(TransactionError::Storage(e)) => return Err(StateDbError::Sled(e)),\n                Err(TransactionError::Abort(e)) => return Err(e),\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Sync to disk.\n        self.db.flush()?;\n\n        // Re-compute and persist new root.\n        let new_root = self.compute_state_root(new_height)?;\n        self.current_height.store(new_height, Ordering::SeqCst);\n\n        // Notify subscribers – ignore send failures (no active listeners).\n        let _ = self\n            .event_tx\n            .send(StateCommitEvent { block_height: new_height, new_state_root: new_root });\n\n        Ok(new_root)\n    }\n\n    /// Deterministically compute the Blake3 root for a given `height`.\n    ///\n    /// The root is the hash of the concatenation of `hash(key) || hash(value)`\n    /// in lexicographic key order.  While *not* a Patricia-Merkle tree, this\n    /// approach provides collision resistance and is fast to compute.\n    pub fn compute_state_root(&self, height: u64) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n        let prefix = height.to_be_bytes();\n        let mut hasher = Hasher::new();\n\n        for kv in self.tree.scan_prefix(prefix) {\n            let (k, v) = kv?;\n            hasher.update(blake3::hash(&k).as_bytes());\n            hasher.update(blake3::hash(&v).as_bytes());\n        }\n        Ok(*hasher.finalize().as_bytes())\n    }\n\n    /// Permanently remove historical states below `keep_from_height`.\n    pub fn prune(&self, keep_from_height: u64) -> Result<(), StateDbError> {\n        {\n            // Exclusive lock to block reads while we delete keys.\n            let _w = self.guard.write();\n            let oldest = self.oldest_height.load(Ordering::SeqCst);\n            if keep_from_height <= oldest {\n                // Nothing to do.\n                return Ok(());\n            }\n\n            // Delete per-key.\n            for kv in self.tree.range(..compose_versioned_key(keep_from_height, b\"\")) {\n                let (k, _) = kv?;\n                self.tree.remove(k)?;\n            }\n            self.db.flush()?;\n            self.oldest_height.store(keep_from_height, Ordering::SeqCst);\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------- High-level helpers for specific Symphony domains ---------- //\n\nimpl StateDb {\n    /// Convenience wrapper around `get` dedicated to `AccountState` lookups.\n    pub fn get_account(&self, address: &Address) -> Result<Option<AccountState>, StateDbError> {\n        self.get::<AccountState>(address)\n    }\n\n    /// Store an entire `AccountState` at the next block height.\n    pub fn set_account(\n        &self,\n        address: &Address,\n        account: &AccountState,\n    ) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n        self.commit_block::<AccountState>(WriteSet::from([WriteOp::Put(\n            address.to_vec(),\n            account.clone(),\n        )]))\n    }\n\n    /// Retrieve arbitrary contract storage key for a given contract `address`.\n    pub fn get_contract_storage<V: DeserializeOwned>(\n        &self,\n        address: &Address,\n        storage_key: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Option<V>, StateDbError> {\n        let mut composite = Vec::from(address.as_slice());\n        composite.extend_from_slice(storage_key);\n        self.get::<V>(&composite)\n    }\n\n    /// Put a value under contract storage.\n    pub fn set_contract_storage<V: Serialize>(\n        &self,\n        address: &Address,\n        storage_key: &[u8],\n        value: &V,\n    ) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n        let mut composite = Vec::from(address.as_slice());\n        composite.extend_from_slice(storage_key);\n        self.commit_block::<V>(WriteSet::from([WriteOp::Put(composite, value.clone())]))\n    }\n}\n\n// ----------------------- Tests ----------------------- //\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use rand::Rng;\n    use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n    fn random_address() -> Address {\n        rand::thread_rng().gen()\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn basic_read_write_account() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().expect(\"tmp dir\");\n        let db = StateDb::open(tmp.path()).unwrap();\n\n        let addr = random_address();\n        let mut acct = AccountState::default();\n        acct.balance = 42;\n\n        let root1 = db.set_account(&addr, &acct).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(db.get_account(&addr).unwrap(), Some(acct.clone()));\n\n        acct.balance = 1337;\n        let root2 = db.set_account(&addr, &acct).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(db.get_account(&addr).unwrap(), Some(acct));\n\n        assert_ne!(root1, root2);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn pruning_works() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let db = StateDb::open(tmp.path()).unwrap();\n        let addr = random_address();\n        let acct = AccountState::default();\n\n        // write 5 blocks\n        for _ in 0..5 {\n }\n}\n```","numLines":409,"startLine":1,"totalLines":409}}}

This file purposefully contains no gRPC or HTTP\n     6→//! plumbing;  it is meant to be embedded by the service‐layer binary and\n     7→//! driven by the surrounding transport / storage adapters.\n     8→//!\n     9→//! Architectural notes\n    10→//! -------------------\n    11→//! • State is kept in-memory behind a `RwLock` and checkpointed by the\n    12→//!   service into persistent storage (e.g. Postgres, RocksDB) – that part is\n    13→//!   out-of-scope here.\n    14→//! • Composer selection follows a simplified Proof-of-Inspiration algorithm:\n    15→//!      1. Each staker submits a *VRF proof* for a given round‐seed.\n    16→//!      2. The hash output is mapped to `[0, 1)`.  The staker wins if\n    17→//!         output / stake_total < staker_weight.\n    18→//!      3. Ties are broken deterministically by lowest hash.\n    19→//! • A Strategy pattern exposes pluggable `VrfProvider`s so that collectives\n    20→//!   can experiment with different curves (Ed25519, 99→/// Abstraction for a Verifiable Random Function provider.\n   100→pub trait VrfProvider: Send + Sync + 'static {\n   101→    /// Produces a VRF proof and output hash for `message` using the 112→    ) -> std::result::Result<(), String>;\n   113→}\n   114→\n   115→/// Fallback VRF provider that is *not* secure; meant only for local-dev and\n   116→/// CI where cryptography isn’t the focus.  It deterministically hashes the\n   117→/// message + static `private_key`.\n   118→pub struct Sha256Vrf {\n   119→    private_key: Vec<u8>,\n   120→}\n   121→\n   122→impl Sha256Vrf {\n   123→    pub fn new() -> Self {\n   124→        let mut pk = vec![0u8; 32];\n   125→        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut pk);\n   126→        Self { private_key: pk }\n   127→    }\n   128→}\n   129→\n   130→impl VrfProvider for Sha256Vrf {\n   131→    fn prove(&self, message: &[u8]) -> (Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>) {\n   132→        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n   133→        hasher.update(&self.private_key);\n   134→        hasher.update(message);\n   135→        let hash = hasher.finalize().to_vec();\n   136→        // `proof` is meaningless here ‑ we return the key for “verification”\n   137→        (self.private_key.clone(), hash)\n   138→    }\n   139→\n   140→    fn verify(\n   141→        &self,\n   142→        public_key: &[u8],\n   143→        message: &[u8],\n   144→        proof: &[u8],\n   145→        hash: &[u8],\n   146→    ) -> std::result::Result<(), String> {\n   147→        if public_key != proof {\n   148→            return Err(\"public key mismatch\".into());\n   149→        }\n   150→        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n   151→        hasher.update(proof); // same as private_key in this dummy impl\n   152→        hasher.update(message);\n   153→        let expected = hasher.finalize();\n   154→        if expected.as_slice() == hash {\n   155→            Ok(())\n   156→        } else {\n   157→            Err(\"hash mismatch\".into())\n   158→        }\n   159→    }\n   160→}\n   161→\n   162→/// Shared staking state guarded by `RwLock`.\n   163→#[derive(Default)]\n   164→struct StakePoolInner {\n   165→    stakers: HashMap<StakerId, Staker>,\n   166→    ) -> Result<()> {\n   201→        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n   202→\n   203→        let entry = inner.stakers.entry(staker_id.clone()).or_insert_with(|| {\n   204→            Staker::new(staker_id.clone(), 0, vrf_pubkey.clone())\n   205→        });\n   206→\n   207→        entry.stake = entry.stake.saturating_add(amount);\n   208→        entry.last_active = Utc::now();\n Fails if the caller tries to withdraw more than they have.\n   221→    pub async fn withdraw_stake(&self, staker_id: &str, amount: u128) -> Result<()> {\n   222→        if amount == 0 {\n   223→ 252→    /// Internal helper to draw a random seed for the next composer round.\n   253→    fn next_round_seed() -> [u8; 32] {\n   254→        let mut seed = [0u8; 32];\n   255→        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut seed);\n   256→        seed\n   257→    }\n   258→\n   259→    /// Schedule automatic composer selection every `interval`.\n   260→    pub async fn run_composer_election(self: Arc<Self>, interval: Duration) {\n   261→        let mut ticker = time::interval(interval);\n   262→\n   263→        loop {\n   264→            ticker.tick().await;\n   265→            let seed = Self::next_round_seed();\n   266→            match self.select_composer(seed).await {\n   267→                Ok(Some(winner)) => {\n   268→                    tracing::info!(round = self.current_round().await, %winner, \"composer selected\");\n   269→                }\n   270→                Ok(None) => {\n   271→                    // pool is empty\n   272→                }\n   273→                Err(e) => tracing::error!(\"failed to select composer: {e}\"),\n   274→            };\n   275→        }\n   276→    }\n   277→\n   278→    /// Return current round number without round with the provided seed.\n   284→    /// Returns `Ok(Some(staker_id))` on success, `Ok(None)` if pool empty.\n   285→    /// State changes (round increment) are committed atomically.\n   286→    pub async fn select_composer(&self, seed: [u8; 32]) -> Result<Option<StakerId>> {\n   287→        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n   288→\n   289→        if inner.stakers.is_empty() {\n   290→            return Ok(None);\n   291→        }\n   292→\n   293→        let message = seed; // serialized seed acts as VRF message\n   294→        let mut best_candidate: Option<(StakerId, Vec<u8>)> = None;\n   295→\n   296→        for staker in inner.stakers.values() {\n   297→            // Build domain-separated message: seed || staker-id\n   298→            let mut msg_vec = message.to_vec();\n   299→            msg_vec.extend(staker.id.as_bytes());\n   300→\n   301→            // Each candidate derives a VRF output (hash) – prove/verify may\n   302→            // happen off-chain in a real system; we shortcut here.\n   303→            let (_proof, hash) = self.vrf.prove(&msg_vec);\n   304→\n   305→            // Map first 16 bytes → u128 for uniform comparison\n   306→            let mut buf = [0u8; 16];\n   307→            buf.copy_from_slice(&hash[..16]);\n   308→            let rnd = u128::from_be_bytes(buf);\n   309→\n   310→            // Calculate target: (rnd / 2^128) < (stake / total)\n   311→            // Reorder: rnd * total < stake * 2^128\n   312→            let left = rnd.saturating_mul(inner.total_stake);\n   313→            let right = staker.stake.saturating_mul(u128::MAX);\n   314→\n   315→            let eligible = left < right;\n   316→            if eligible {\n   317→                // Keep the lowest hash to break ties deterministically\n   318→                match &mut best_candidate {\n   319→                    None => best_candidate = Some((staker.id.clone(), hash)),\n   320→                    Some((_best_id, best_hash)) => {\n   321→                        if hash < *best_hash {\n   322→                            *best_hash = hash;\n   323→                            *_best_id = staker.id.clone();\n   324→                        }\n   325→                    }\n   326→                }\n   327→            }\n   328→        }\n   329→\n   330→        inner.current_round += 1;\n   331→\n   332→        if let Some((winner, _)) = &best_candidate {\n   333→            self.event_tx\n   334→                .send(StakingEvent::ComposerSelected {\n   335→                    round: inner.current_round,\n   336→                    staker: winner.clone(),\n   337→                })\n   338→                .ok();\n   339→        }\n   340→\n   341→        Ok(best_candidate.map(|(id, _)| id))\n   342→    }\n   343→\n   344→    /// Utility used primarily by tests & admin dashboards.\n   345→    pub async fn snapshot(&self) -> (u128, Vec<Staker>) {\n   346→        let inner = self.inner.read().await;\n Core staking & composer–selection logic for the CanvasChain Symphony\n//! staking micro-service.  This file purposefully contains no gRPC or HTTP\n//! plumbing;  it is meant to be embedded by the service‐layer binary and\n//! driven by the surrounding transport / storage adapters.\n//!\n//! Architectural notes\n//! -------------------\n//! • State is kept in-memory behind a `RwLock` and checkpointed by the\n//!   service into persistent storage (e.g. Postgres, RocksDB) – that part is\n//!   out-of-scope here.\n//! • Composer selection follows a simplified Proof-of-Inspiration algorithm:\n//!      1. Each staker submits a *VRF proof* for a given round‐seed.\n//!      2. The hash output is mapped to `[0, 1)`.  The staker wins if\n//!         output / stake_total < staker_weight.\n//!      3. Ties are broken deterministically by lowest hash.\n//! • A Strategy pattern exposes pluggable `VrfProvider`s so that collectives\n//!   can experiment with different curves (Ed25519, BLS, PQ, …) without\n//!   recompilation of the other micro-services.\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::{Duration, SystemTime},\n};\n\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse rand::{rngs::OsRng, RngCore};\nuse sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\nuse tokio::{\n    sync::{broadcast, RwLock},\n    time,\n};\n\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// Unique user/staker identifier (e.g. wallet address).\npub type StakerId = String;\n/// Result shorthand used throughout the stake logic.\npub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, StakingError>;\n\n/// Public event types that can be subscribed to by other micro-services\n/// through the event-bus (`broadcast::Sender`).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum StakingEvent {\n    StakeAdded {\n        staker: StakerId,\n        amount: u128,\n        new_total: u128,\n    },\n    StakeWithdrawn {\n        staker: StakerId,\n        amount: u128,\n        new_total: u128,\n    },\n    ComposerSelected {\n        round: u64,\n        staker: StakerId,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Domain errors emitted by staking logic.\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum StakingError {\n    #[error(\"insufficient stake: requested {requested}, available {available}\")]\n    InsufficientStake { requested: u128, available: u128 },\n    #[error(\"staker `{0}` is not registered\")]\n    UnknownStaker(StakerId),\n    #[error(\"VRF verification failed: {0}\")]\n    VrfFailed(String),\n    #[error(\"no stakers registered\")]\n    EmptyPool,\n    #[error(\"attempted to withdraw zero tokens\")]\n    ZeroWithdrawal,\n}\n\n/// Represents an individual staking participant.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Staker {\n    pub id: StakerId,\n    pub stake: u128,\n    pub last_active: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>, // curve is provider specific\n}\n\nimpl Staker {\n    fn new(id: StakerId, initial: u128, vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id,\n            stake: initial,\n            last_active: Utc::now(),\n            vrf_pubkey,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Abstraction for a Verifiable Random Function provider.\npub trait VrfProvider: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    /// Produces a VRF proof and output hash for `message` using the *private*\n    /// part of the provider implementation.\n    fn prove(&self, message: &[u8]) -> (Vec<u8> /*proof*/, Vec<u8> /*hash*/);\n\n    /// Verify a proof & hash produced by `prove`.\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public_key: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        proof: &[u8],\n        hash: &[u8],\n    ) -> std::result::Result<(), String>;\n}\n\n/// Fallback VRF provider that is *not* secure; meant only for local-dev and\n/// CI where cryptography isn’t the focus.  It deterministically hashes the\n/// message + static `private_key`.\npub struct Sha256Vrf {\n    private_key: Vec<u8>,\n}\n\nimpl Sha256Vrf {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let mut pk = vec![0u8; 32];\n        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut pk);\n        Self { private_key: pk }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl VrfProvider for Sha256Vrf {\n    fn prove(&self, message: &[u8]) -> (Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>) {\n        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n        hasher.update(&self.private_key);\n        hasher.update(message);\n        let hash = hasher.finalize().to_vec();\n        // `proof` is meaningless here ‑ we return the key for “verification”\n        (self.private_key.clone(), hash)\n    }\n\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public_key: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        proof: &[u8],\n        hash: &[u8],\n    ) -> std::result::Result<(), String> {\n        if public_key != proof {\n            return Err(\"public key mismatch\".into());\n        }\n        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n        hasher.update(proof); // same as private_key in this dummy impl\n        hasher.update(message);\n        let expected = hasher.finalize();\n        if expected.as_slice() == hash {\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            Err(\"hash mismatch\".into())\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Shared staking state guarded by `RwLock`.\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct StakePoolInner {\n    stakers: HashMap<StakerId, Staker>,\n    total_stake: u128,\n    current_round: u64,\n}\n\n/// Public handle for performing staking operations + composer selection.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct StakePool {\n    inner: Arc<RwLock<StakePoolInner>>,\n    vrf: Arc<dyn VrfProvider>,\n    event_tx: broadcast::Sender<StakingEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl StakePool {\n    /// Create a new [`StakePool`] with the provided `VrfProvider`.\n    pub fn new(vrf: Arc<dyn VrfProvider>) -> Self {\n        let (event_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(256);\n        Self {\n            inner: Arc::default(),\n            vrf,\n            event_tx,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to [`StakingEvent`]s.\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<StakingEvent> {\n        self.event_tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    /// Register a new staker or top-up their existing stake.\n    pub async fn add_stake(\n        &self,\n        staker_id: StakerId,\n        vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>,\n        amount: u128,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n\n        let entry = inner.stakers.entry(staker_id.clone()).or_insert_with(|| {\n            Staker::new(staker_id.clone(), 0, vrf_pubkey.clone())\n        });\n\n        entry.stake = entry.stake.saturating_add(amount);\n        entry.last_active = Utc::now();\n        inner.total_stake = inner.total_stake.saturating_add(amount);\n\n        self.event_tx.send(StakingEvent::StakeAdded {\n            staker: staker_id,\n            amount,\n            new_total: entry.stake,\n        }).ok();\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Withdraw stake.  Fails if the caller tries to withdraw more than they have.\n    pub async fn withdraw_stake(&self, staker_id: &str, amount: u128) -> Result<()> {\n        if amount == 0 {\n            return Err(StakingError::ZeroWithdrawal);\n        }\n\n        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n        let staker = inner\n            .stakers\n            .get_mut(staker_id)\n            .ok_or_else(|| StakingError::UnknownStaker(staker_id.into()))?;\n\n        if staker.stake < amount {\n            return Err(StakingError::InsufficientStake {\n                requested: amount,\n                available: staker.stake,\n            });\n        }\n\n        staker.stake -= amount;\n        staker.last_active = Utc::now();\n        inner.total_stake -= amount;\n\n        self.event_tx.send(StakingEvent::StakeWithdrawn {\n            staker: staker_id.into(),\n            amount,\n            new_total: staker.stake,\n        }).ok();\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Internal helper to draw a random seed for the next composer round.\n    fn next_round_seed() -> [u8; 32] {\n        let mut seed = [0u8; 32];\n        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut seed);\n        seed\n    }\n\n    /// Schedule automatic composer selection every `interval`.\n    pub async fn run_composer_election(self: Arc<Self>, interval: Duration) {\n        let mut ticker = time::interval(interval);\n\n        loop {\n            ticker.tick().await;\n            let seed = Self::next_round_seed();\n            match self.select_composer(seed).await {\n                Ok(Some(winner)) => {\n                    tracing::info!(round = self.current_round().await, %winner, \"composer selected\");\n                }\n                Ok(None) => {\n                    // pool is empty\n                }\n                Err(e) => tracing::error!(\"failed to select composer: {e}\"),\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Return current round number without locking write.\n    pub async fn current_round(&self) -> u64 {\n        self.inner.read().await.current_round\n    }\n\n    /// Execute a single composer election round with the provided seed.\n    /// Returns `Ok(Some(staker_id))` on success, `Ok(None)` if pool empty.\n    /// State changes (round increment) are committed atomically.\n    pub async fn select_composer(&self, seed: [u8; 32]) -> Result<Option<StakerId>> {\n        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n\n        if inner.stakers.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n\n        let message = seed; // serialized seed acts as VRF message\n        let mut best_candidate: Option<(StakerId, Vec<u8>)> = None;\n\n        for staker in inner.stakers.values() {\n            // Build domain-separated message: seed || staker-id\n            let mut msg_vec = message.to_vec();\n            msg_vec.extend(staker.id.as_bytes());\n\n            // Each candidate derives a VRF output (hash) – prove/verify may\n            // happen off-chain in a real system; we shortcut here.\n            let (_proof, hash) = self.vrf.prove(&msg_vec);\n\n            // Map first 16 bytes → u128 for uniform comparison\n            let mut buf = [0u8; 16];\n            buf.copy_from_slice(&hash[..16]);\n            let rnd = u128::from_be_bytes(buf);\n\n            // Calculate target: (rnd / 2^128) < (stake / total)\n            // Reorder: rnd * total < stake * 2^128\n            let left = rnd.saturating_mul(inner.total_stake);\n            let right = staker.stake.saturating_mul(u128::MAX);\n\n            let eligible = left < right;\n            if eligible {\n                // Keep the lowest hash to break ties deterministically\n                match &mut best_candidate {\n                    None => best_candidate = Some((staker.id.clone(), hash)),\n                    Some((_best_id, best_hash)) => {\n                        if hash < *best_hash {\n                            *best_hash = hash;\n                            *_best_id = staker.id.clone();\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        inner.current_round += 1;\n\n        if let Some((winner, _)) = &best_candidate {\n            self.event_tx\n                .send(StakingEvent::ComposerSelected {\n                    round: inner.current_round,\n                    staker: winner.clone(),\n                })\n                .ok();\n        }\n\n        Ok(best_candidate.map(|(id, _)| id))\n    }\n\n    /// Utility used primarily by tests & admin dashboards.\n    pub async fn snapshot(&self) -> (u128, Vec<Staker>) {\n        let inner = self.inner.read().await;\n        (inner.total_stake, inner.stakers.values().cloned().collect())\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::collections::HashSet;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_stake_add_withdraw() 2_000).await.unwrap();\n        let (total, _) = pool.snapshot().await;\n        assert_eq!(total, 3_000);\n\n        pool.withdraw_stake(\"bob\", 500).await.unwrap();\n        let (total, stakers) = pool.snapshot().await;\n        assert_eq!(total, 2_500);\n\n        let bob_stake = stakers.iter().find(|s| s.id == \"bob\").unwrap().stake;\n        assert_eq!(bob_stake, 1_500);\n    }\n\n The F-NFT feature will allow high-value multi-layer NFTs to be \"sharded\" into multiple fungible tokens, enabling fractional ownership, increased liquidity, and broader accessibility to premium digital art assets.\n\nThe design follows the existing microservices architecture using gRPC for inter-service communication and NATS JetStream for event-driven messaging, as established in ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n---\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n1. [Impact Analysis](#1-impact-analysis)\n2. [Data Model Changes](#2-data-model-changes)\n3. [Component-Level Design](#3-component-level-design)\n4. [Core User Flows](#4-core-user-flows)\n5. [API & Configuration Changes](#5-api--configuration-changes)\n6. [Security Considerations](#6-security-considerations)\n7. [Migration Strategy](#7-migration-strategy)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Impact Analysis\n\n### 1.1 Impacted Services\n\n| Service | Impact Level | Description |\n|---------|--------------|-------------|\n| `node_service` | **Critical** | New transaction types for fractionalization and fraction transfers |\n| `minting_service` | **High** | New fractionalization endpoint, fraction token creation logic |\n| `wallet_service` | **High** | Display and manage fractional ownership positions |\n| `marketplace_service` | **High** | List, bid, and trade fraction tokens |\n| `governance_service` | **Medium** | Weighted voting based on fractional ownership |\n| `staking_service` | **Medium** | Staking rewards distribution for fraction holders |\n| `api_gateway` | **High** | New REST endpoints for F-NFT operations |\n| `royalty_service` | **Medium** | Royalty distribution to fraction holders |\n\n### 1.2 Impacted Shared Crates\n\n| Crate | Impact Level | Changes Required |\n|-------|--------------|------------------|\n| `ccs_common` | **Critical** | New `FractionalInfo` struct, `FractionalNft` type, modified `MultiLayerNft` |\n| `ccs_proto` | **High** | New protobuf messages for F-NFT operations |\n| `ccs_db` | **High** | New state storage for fractional ownership records |\n| `ccs_consensus` | **Medium** | Validation rules for fractionalization transactions |\n| `ccs_crypto` | **Low** | No changes (existing signature schemes sufficient) |\n\n### 1.3 Critical Files Impacted\n\n#### Core Domain (ccs_common)\n- `crates/ccs_common/src/nft.rs` - **PRIMARY CHANGE POINT**: Add `FractionalInfo` struct to `MultiLayerNft`\n- `crates/ccs_common/src/types.rs` - Add `FractionTokenId`, `FractionBalance` types\n- `crates/ccs_common/src/error.rs` - Add F-NFT specific error variants\n\n#### Node Service\n- `services/node_service/src/state_machine.rs` - Add `Transaction::Fractionalize`, `Transaction::TransferFraction` handling\n- `services/node_service/src/mempool.rs` - Validate fractionalization transactions\n- `services/node_service/src/rpc_server.rs` - New RPC methods for F-NFT queries\n\n#### Minting Service\n- `services/minting_service/src/logic/nft_factory.rs` - Add `fractionalize_nft()` method\n- `services/minting_service/src/rpc_server.rs` - New gRPC endpoints\n- `services/minting_service/src/logic/mod.rs` - Coordinate fractionalization logic\n\n#### Marketplace Service\n- `services/marketplace_service/src/listing_manager.rs` - Support fraction listings\n- `services/marketplace_service/src/auction_engine.rs` - Auction fractional tokens\n\n#### Wallet Service\n- `services/wallet_service/src/key_manager.rs` - Manage fraction token balances\n- `services/wallet_service/src/rpc_server.rs` - Query fractional holdings\n\n#### Governance Service\n- `services/governance_service/src/proposal_engine.rs` - Weighted voting calculations\n- `services/governance_service/src/proposal_state.rs` - Track fraction-based voting power\n\n#### API Gateway\n- `services/api_gateway/src/rest_api.rs` - New F-NFT REST endpoints\n- `services/api_gateway/src/grpc_client.rs` - gRPC client calls for F-NFT operations\n\n#### Shared Protocol Definitions\n- `crates/ccs_proto/src/lib.rs` - New protobuf message definitions\n\n#### State Storage\n- `crates/ccs_db/src/state_db.rs` - Fractional ownership state persistence\n\n---\n\n## 2. Data Model Changes\n\n### 2.1 Core Data Structures (ccs_common/src/nft.rs)\n\n#### New Struct: `FractionalInfo`\n\n```rust\n/// Metadata for a fractionalized NFT.\n/// This struct is attached to a `MultiLayerNft` when it has been sharded.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionalInfo {\n    /// Unique identifier for the fraction token series\n    pub fraction_token_id: Uuid,\n\n    /// Total number of fractions (shares) created\n    pub total_supply: u64,\n\n    /// Current circulating supply (may differ if some burned)\n    pub circulating_supply: u64,\n\n    /// Minimum fraction units that can be traded (e.g., 1 = indivisible fractions)\n    pub min_trade_unit: u64,\n\n    /// Timestamp when fractionalization occurred\n    pub fractionalized_at: u64,\n\n    /// Address that initiated the fractionalization\n    pub fractionalizer: WalletAddress,\n\n    /// Whether the NFT can be reconstituted (all fractions returned to one owner)\n    pub reconstitutable: bool,\n\n    /// Buyout price in platform tokens (optional, for forced buyout mechanism)\n    pub buyout_price: Option<TokenAmount>,\n\n    /// Current state of the fractional NFT\n    pub state: FractionalState,\n}\n\n/// State machine for fractional NFT lifecycle\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum FractionalState {\n    /// Fractionalization is pending (in mempool)\n    Pending,\n    /// NFT is actively fractionalized\n    Active,\n    /// Buyout initiated, fractions can be redeemed\n    BuyoutInProgress { initiator: WalletAddress, deadline: u64 },\n    /// NFT has been reconstituted to a single owner\n    Reconstituted,\n    /// Fractionalized NFT has been dissolved (rare edge case)\n    Dissolved,\n}\n```\n\n#### Modified Struct: `MultiLayerNft`\n\n```rust\n/// The main multi-layer NFT object with optional fractionalization.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct MultiLayerNft {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub name: String,\n    pub creator: WalletAddress,\n    pub layers: BTreeMap<Uuid, NftLayer>,\n    pub governance: GovernancePolicy,\n    pub royalties: RoyaltySchedule,\n\n    // NEW FIELD: Fractionalization metadata (None if not fractionalized)\n    pub fractional: Option<FractionalInfo>,\n}\n```\n\n#### New Struct: `FractionToken`\n\n```rust\n/// Represents a holder's fractional ownership position\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionToken {\n    /// Reference to the parent NFT's fraction_token_id\n    pub parent_token_id: Uuid,\n\n    /// Owner's wallet address\n    pub owner: WalletAddress,\n\n    /// Number of fraction units owned\n    pub balance: u64,\n\n    /// Last transfer/acquisition timestamp\n    pub last_updated: u64,\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 New Error Variants (ccs_common/src/error.rs)\n\n```rust\n/// Add to NftError enum:\n#[error(\"NFT is already fractionalized: {0}\")]\nAlreadyFractionalized(Uuid),\n\n#[error(\"cannot fractionalize frozen NFT: {0}\")]\nCannotFractionalizeFrozen(Uuid),\n\n#[error(\"insufficient fraction balance: have {available}, need {requested}\")]\nInsufficientFractions { available: u64, requested: u64 },\n\n#[error(\"fraction transfer not allowed: {0}\")]\nFractionTransferBlocked(String),\n\n#[error(\"buyout price not met: required {required}, offered {offered}\")]\nBuyoutPriceNotMet { required: TokenAmount, offered: TokenAmount },\n\n#[error(\"reconstitution requires 100% ownership\")]\nIncompleteReconstitution,\n```\n\n### 2.3 Protocol Buffer Definitions (ccs_proto/src/lib.rs)\n\n```protobuf\n// New module: pub mod fractional\npub mod fractional {\n    use super::*;\n\n    /// Request to fractionalize an existing NFT\n    #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Message)]\n    pub struct FractionalizeRequest {\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"1\")]\n        pub nft_id: String,\n\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"2\")]\n        pub owner_wallet: String,\n\n        #[prost(uint64, tag = \"3\")]\n        pub total_fractions: u64,\n\n        #[prost(uint64, tag = \"4\")]\n        pub min_trade_unit: u64,\n\n        #[prost(bool, tag = \"5\")]\n        pub reconstitutable: bool,\n\n        #[prost(uint64, optional, tag = \"6\")]\n        pub buyout_price: Option<u64>,\n\n        #[prost(bytes = \"vec\", tag = \"7\")]\n        pub signature: Vec<u8>,\n    }\n\n    /// Response after successful fractionalization\n    #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Message)]\n    pub struct FractionalizeResponse {\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"1\")]\n        pub fraction_token_id: String,\n\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"2\")]\n        pub tx_hash: String,\n\n        #[prost(bool, tag = \"3\")]\n        pub success: bool,\n    }\n\n    /// Query fraction holdings for a wallet\n    #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Message)]\n    pub struct QueryFractionsRequest {\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"1\")]\n        pub wallet_address: String,\n\n        #[prost(string, optional, tag = \"2\")]\n        pub nft_id_filter: Option<String>,\n    }\n\n    /// Fraction holding record\n    #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Message)]\n    pub struct FractionHolding {\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"1\")]\n        pub nft_id: String,\n\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"2\")]\n        pub fraction_token_id: String,\n\n        #[prost(uint64, tag = \"3\")]\n        pub balance: u64,\n\n        #[prost(uint64, tag = \"4\")]\n        pub total_supply: u64,\n\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"5\")]\n        pub nft_name: String,\n    }\n\n    /// Transfer fractions between wallets\n    #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Message)]\n    pub struct TransferFractionsRequest {\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"1\")]\n        pub fraction_token_id: String,\n\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"2\")]\n        pub from_wallet: String,\n\n        #[prost(string, tag = \"3\")]\n        pub to_wallet: String,\n\n        #[prost(uint64, tag = \"4\")]\n        pub amount: u64,\n\n        #[prost(bytes = \"vec\", tag = \"5\")]\n        pub signature: Vec<u8>,\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.4 State Database Changes (ccs_db/src/state_db.rs)\n\n```rust\n// New storage keys and methods to add:\n\nimpl StateDb {\n    /// Key prefix for fraction ownership records\n    const FRACTION_PREFIX: &'static [u8] = b\"frac:\";\n\n    /// Key prefix for fraction metadata\n    const FRACTION_META_PREFIX: &'static [u8] = b\"frac_meta:\";\n\n    /// Store fraction balance for a wallet\n    pub fn set_fraction_balance(\n        &self,\n        fraction_token_id: &[u8; 32],\n        wallet: &Address,\n        balance: u64,\n    ) -> Result<StateRoot, StateDbError> {\n        let key = self.compose_fraction_key(fraction_token_id, wallet);\n        self.commit_block(WriteSet::from([WriteOp::Put(key, balance)]))\n    }\n\n    /// Get fraction balance for a wallet\n    pub fn get_fraction_balance(\n        &self,\n        fraction_token_id: &[u8; 32],\n        wallet: &Address,\n    ) -> Result<Option<u64>, StateDbError> {\n        let key = self.compose_fraction_key(fraction_token_id, wallet);\n        self.get::<u64>(&key)\n    }\n\n    /// List all fraction holders for a token\n    pub fn list_fraction_holders(\n        &self,\n        fraction_token_id: &[u8; 32],\n    ) -> Result<Vec<(Address, u64)>, StateDbError> {\n        // Scan with prefix to find all holders\n        let prefix = self.compose_fraction_prefix(fraction_token_id);\n        // Implementation details...\n    }\n\n    fn compose_fraction_key(&self, token_id: &[u8; 32], wallet: &Address) -> Vec<u8> {\n        let mut key = Self::FRACTION_PREFIX.to_vec();\n        key.extend_from_slice(token_id);\n        key.extend_from_slice(wallet.as_bytes());\n        key\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Component-Level Design\n\n### 3.1 Node Service Changes\n\n#### File: `services/node_service/src/state_machine.rs`\n\n**New Transaction Types:**\n\n```rust\n/// Extended NodeEvent enum with F-NFT events\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum NodeEvent {\n    // Existing events...\n    Tick,\n    PeerConnected(PeerId),\n    BlockReceived(Block),\n    ElectedComposer,\n    Shutdown,\n\n    // NEW: Fractionalization events\n    FractionalizeNft {\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        owner: WalletAddress,\n        total_fractions: u64,\n        min_trade_unit: u64,\n        signature: Vec<u8>,\n    },\n    TransferFractions {\n        fraction_token_id: Uuid,\n        from: WalletAddress,\n        to: WalletAddress,\n        amount: u64,\n        signature: Vec<u8>,\n    },\n    InitiateBuyout {\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        initiator: WalletAddress,\n        offered_price: TokenAmount,\n    },\n    ReconstituteFractionalNft {\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        owner: WalletAddress, // Must own 100% of fractions\n    },\n}\n```\n\n**State Transition Logic:**\n\nAdd to `StateActive::on_event`:\n\n```rust\n// In StateActive implementation\nasync fn on_event(&mut self, ctx: &mut NodeContext, event: NodeEvent) -> anyhow::Result<Transition> {\n    match event {\n        // ... existing handlers ...\n\n        NodeEvent::FractionalizeNft { nft_id, owner, total_fractions, min_trade_unit, signature } => {\n            // 1. Verify ownership\n            // 2. Verify NFT is not already fractionalized\n            // 3. Verify NFT layers are not frozen (or handle appropriately)\n            // 4. Create FractionalInfo\n            // 5. Mint fraction tokens to owner\n            // 6. Update state and publish event\n            handle_fractionalize(ctx, nft_id, owner, total_fractions, min_trade_unit, signature).await?;\n            Ok(Transition::Stay)\n        }\n\n        NodeEvent::TransferFractions { fraction_token_id, from, to, amount, signature } => {\n            // 1. Verify signature\n            // 2. Check balance\n            // 3. Execute transfer\n            // 4. Emit event\n            handle_fraction_transfer(ctx, fraction_token_id, from, to, amount, signature).await?;\n            Ok(Transition::Stay)\n        }\n        // ... more handlers\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### File: `services/node_service/src/mempool.rs`\n\nAdd validation for fractionalization transactions:\n\n```rust\nimpl Mempool {\n    /// Validate a fractionalization transaction before adding to mempool\n    pub fn validate_fractionalize_tx(\n        &self,\n        nft_id: &Uuid,\n        owner: &WalletAddress,\n        total_fractions: u64,\n    ) -> Result<(), MempoolError> {\n        // 1. Minimum fractions check (e.g., >= 2)\n        if total_fractions < 2 {\n            return Err(MempoolError::InvalidFractionCount);\n        }\n\n        // 2. Maximum fractions check (prevent gas attacks)\n        if total_fractions > 1_000_000_000 {\n            return Err(MempoolError::ExcessiveFractionCount);\n        }\n\n        // 3. Check NFT exists and is owned by sender\n        // 4. Check NFT is not already fractionalized\n        // 5. Check no pending fractionalize tx for this NFT\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 Minting Service Changes\n\n#### File: `services/minting_service/src/logic/nft_factory.rs`\n\n**New Methods:**\n\n```rust\nimpl<S, C, E> NftFactory<S, C, E>\nwhere\n    S: CryptoSigner + 'static,\n    C: BlockchainClient + 'static,\n    E: EventPublisher + 'static,\n{\n    /// Fractionalize an existing NFT into fungible fraction tokens\n    #[instrument(skip_all, level = \"info\", fields(nft_id = %req.nft_id))]\n    pub async fn fractionalize_nft(&self, req: FractionalizeRequest) -> Result<FractionalizeResponse, FactoryError> {\n        // 1. Validate the request\n        self.validate_fractionalize_request(&req)?;\n\n        // 2. Build fractionalization payload\n        let fraction_token_id = Uuid::new_v4();\n        let payload = self.build_fractionalize_payload(&req, &fraction_token_id).await?;\n\n        // 3. Sign the payload\n        let signature = self.signer.sign(&payload).await?;\n\n        // 4. Submit to chain\n        let tx_hash = self.chain.submit_fractionalize(FractionalizePayload {\n            nft_id: req.nft_id.clone(),\n            fraction_token_id: fraction_token_id.to_string(),\n            total_fractions: req.total_fractions,\n            min_trade_unit: req.min_trade_unit,\n            reconstitutable: req.reconstitutable,\n            buyout_price: req.buyout_price,\n            signature,\n        }).await?;\n\n        // 5. Publish success event\n        self.bus.publish(\n            &self.config.event_topic_fractionalized,\n            &FractionalizedEvent {\n                nft_id: req.nft_id.clone(),\n                fraction_token_id: fraction_token_id.to_string(),\n                total_fractions: req.total_fractions,\n                owner: req.owner_wallet.clone(),\n                timestamp_ms: now_millis(),\n            },\n        ).await?;\n\n        Ok(FractionalizeResponse {\n            fraction_token_id: fraction_token_id.to_string(),\n            tx_hash,\n            success: true,\n        })\n    }\n\n    fn validate_fractionalize_request(&self, req: &FractionalizeRequest) -> Result<(), FactoryError> {\n        if req.total_fractions < 2 {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"must create at least 2 fractions\".into()));\n        }\n        if req.total_fractions > self.config.max_fractions {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(format!(\n                \"cannot exceed {} fractions\",\n                self.config.max_fractions\n            )));\n        }\n        if req.min_trade_unit == 0 || req.min_trade_unit > req.total_fractions {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"invalid min_trade_unit\".into()));\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### File: `services/minting_service/src/rpc_server.rs`\n\n**New gRPC Endpoint:**\n\n```rust\n/// gRPC service implementation for fractionalization\n#[tonic::async_trait]\nimpl FractionalService for MintingRpcServer {\n    async fn fractionalize(\n        &self,\n        request: Request<FractionalizeRequest>,\n    ) -> Result<Response<FractionalizeResponse>, Status> {\n        let req = request.into_inner();\n\n        // Validate signature\n        self.verify_signature(&req.owner_wallet, &req.signature).await\n            .map_err(|e| Status::unauthenticated(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        // Execute fractionalization\n        let response = self.factory\n            .fractionalize_nft(req)\n            .await\n            .map_err(|e| Status::internal(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        Ok(Response::new(response))\n    }\n\n    async fn reconstitute(\n        &self,\n        request: Request<ReconstituteRequest>,\n    ) -> Result<Response<ReconstituteResponse>, Status> {\n        // Handle NFT reconstitution when one owner has all fractions\n        // ...\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Wallet Service Changes\n\n#### File: `services/wallet_service/src/key_manager.rs`\n\n**Extended id: Uuid,\n    pub algo: CryptoAlgorithm,\n    pub public_key: Vec<u8>,\n    pub encrypted_sk: Vec<u8>,\n    pub enc_salt: Vec<u8>,\n    pub enc_nonce: Vec<u8>,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n\n    // NEW: Cached fraction holdings (for quick UI display)\n    pub fraction_holdings: Vec<FractionHoldingSummary>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct
